### Deferred item tags

Each deferred item should carry one of these tags so future agents know the *risk* of the PR it warrants:

- `[trust-critical]` — could silently corrupt data, produce wrong numbers, or break the preview→approve→write path.
- `[correctness]` — wrong behavior visible to user but no data corruption risk.
- `[polish]` — wording, ordering, or UX quality. No behavior change.
- `[housekeeping]` — dead code, duplicate logic, test gaps, doc cleanup.

> **Implementation model:** no builder model is mandatory. Model suggestions retained in older item records are historical risk/context notes only, never an active model or merge gate.

### Open follow-ups

- `[polish]` **AI-voiced on-plan wrap line** (Issue #1073, Phase 1 follow-up). The completed-on-plan-block acknowledgement (`templatedOnPlanWrapLine` in `src/app/coachVoiceTemplates.js`) is a deterministic, data-grounded line. The owner PASSED the Phase 1 Soul gate (2026-07-20) with it but the AI voice would read warmer — optionally route a completed on-plan block through the coach model for a brief model-authored line, with the deterministic grounded line as the outage fallback; weigh the per-set cost/latency.
- `[correctness]` **Coach the finishing set of a per-set-logged exercise** (Issue #1073, Phase 1 follow-up). The on-plan wrap line currently fires only on a batch (`todaySets.length >= 2`); a per-set-logged exercise's finishing set stays silent because `getSessionCompleted()` is eager. Gate it on the canonical per-slot set count (`planSlotStatuses` performed vs required) so the finishing set is coached without becoming chatty on intermediate sets.
- `[correctness]` **`atlas:status` should reflect the campaign PHASE, not a superseded F-card** (Recovery Campaign, Phase 2 status/manifest work). `parseActivePlanCard` (`services/atlasStatus.js`) selects the first non-COMPLETE F-card as `active_card`; under the campaign the M-milestone F-cards are superseded and the active work is the campaign phase (the `CAMPAIGN STATE` "Next step"). So `atlas:status` reports the Phase-2 milestone while selecting a passed gate (e.g. `F10S-GATE`) as the active card. Make the status contract campaign-phase aware so an operator can't mistake a passed gate for current work.

- `[polish]` **Plan-complete pin counts the whole session ("Overhead Press — 9 sets in") (owner housekeeping, 2026-07-18).** After every planned slot completes, `renderSessionPin` deliberately falls back to the session-total count with the last logged lift (the F10S4 plan-complete branch) — the gate rerun's final state showed "Overhead Press · 9 sets in", which reads as if OHP had 9 sets. Small polish: a completed-plan pin should say something like "Session done — 9 sets" (no lift attribution) instead of pairing the total with one lift's name. Owner-directed: do NOT fold into F10D unless it affects closeout truth or proof (it does not — display only).
- ✅ `[correctness]` **Extra next-up handoff under slow-runner interleaving — ROOT-CAUSED and FIXED (gate-hardening PR, 2026-07-18).** The original check-then-act-duplicate hypothesis was wrong (the announce block is synchronous — no dup path exists). Real mechanism: every per-set `handleSetLogged` handler derives `nextEx = remaining[0]` asynchronously after its coach awaits, and F10S1's slot semantics keep an under-count slot at `remaining[0]` — so a handler deriving MID-SLOT announced the lift the lifter was already on ("Moving on — next up: Romanian Deadlift." after RDL set 1), with the card's landing time (and hence which checkpoint saw it) a runner-speed lottery. That was the CI 3-vs-2 count and, once the gate asserted per-card content, a reproducible-locally wrong card. Fix: the `stillOnLoggedSlot` guard in the handoff path (`src/app/coach-conversation.js`) — never announce the just-logged lift while its own slot is still the live `remaining[0]`; boundary handoffs (including single-set slots), off-plan "back to the plan" nudges, and reroutes all still announce. The F10S-GATE spec now asserts exact per-boundary counts + per-card content + order. Display-layer only; no data risk.
- `[polish]` **LLM-down "what's next" answers the generic fallback, not the deterministic next-up (F10S-GATE rerun discovery, 2026-07-18).** With the coach LLM unavailable, a mid-session "what's next" has no deterministic server lane: `/api/coach/chat`'s unconfigured path (`buildSessionCloseAnswer` → `buildSessionQuestionAnswer` → `buildSessionAdviceFallback`) answers attribute/close/advice shapes but not the next-up ask, so the client degrades to the generic "keep logging" chat fallback. The engine KNOWS next-up (`plan_state.remaining[0]` travels in context — the F10S3 lane; bare shorthand like "how much?" already answers from it, which is how the gate rerun proved agreement). Smallest fix when picked up: answer "what('s| is)? next" from `plan_state.remaining[0]` in the deterministic chat lanes, mirroring `answerBareShorthand`'s current-lift resolution. Never a guess today — the fallback names no lift — so this is polish, not a trust defect.
- 🚨 **2026-07-18 owner Work-mode smoke test — FAILED stabilization gate (F10D paused; sequenced by the owner-directed insertion F10S1–F10S6 + F10S-GATE in [`docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`](./docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md) — awareness list, NOT a competing queue).** Findings, in owner priority order (canonical cards hold the reproduce steps): F10S1 one RDL set completed the whole 3-set RDL slot (completion must respect required set count per `plan_item_id`); F10S2 a Front Squat "instead of Back Squat" substitution did not satisfy the original slot (Back Squat stayed current); F10S3 chat next-up (Overhead Press) disagreed with the TODAY rail (Back Squat) — one canonical selector for every consumer; F10S4 the pin showed "Back Squat · 4 sets in" counting total session sets, not the current item's; F10S5 closeout duplicated the same substitution note three times; F10S6 "What should I do today?" fell into the logging fallback, "Romanian Deadlift 245 x 6 @3" failed to log, and "Front Squat 185 7/2 x3 instead of Back Squat" failed to log+bind in one turn. Positive: closeout preserved actuals and never overwrote the original plan (the two-truths foundation held). Exit: the F10S-GATE non-destructive rerun must pass (in-progress multiplicity, substitution binds, surfaces agree, NL inputs work, dedup) with transcript + screenshots returned before F10D authorization.
- ✅ `[trust-critical]` **`F10B-REVISION-WIRING-1` — FIXED (F10B slice 2, 2026-07-18).** Wired the mid-session explicit-revision TRIGGER to `POST /api/session-plan-sets/revision`, still dry-run. New pure client ledger `src/app/sessionLedger.js`: `performedSetCount` (the frozen floor), `buildFutureRevisions` (one revision per FUTURE set only — performed sets frozen; never fabricated — an exact `target_weight` AND `target_reps` are required, bodyweight `0` preserved; idempotent per identical re-trigger, next linear version on a genuine change), `appendRevisions` (append-only). `src/app/app.js` `emitFutureSetRevision` is called from exactly one site — the explicit `applySessionSubstitution` path — so a logged set (`emitSetLogged`) can never create a revision (proven structurally in `test/sessionRevisionWiring.test.js`). Revisions persist in a new `_sessionRevisions` store signal (snapshot shape v2→v3) and reconstruct on reload; server derives `supersedes_key` from version N-1 (`services/sessionPlanLedger.js` `supersedesKeyFor`). Red-first `test/sessionLedger.test.js` + `test/sessionRevisionWiring.test.js`. Full suite 5668 pass. Canonical card: F10B (COMPLETE) in [`docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`](./docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md).

  - ✅ `[trust-critical]` **`FR-REPLAY-1` — FIXED (F09B, 2026-07-17).** Production v141 Flight Recorder captured only server `api_response` rows because the client recorder gated activation on the raw `localStorage['atlas_api_key']`, which the F04C cookie migration removes → inert for a cookie-authenticated owner (no client events; server rows unlinked). Fixed: activation authenticates via the same-origin session cookie; also enriched the session-state snapshot to capture the bounded pending session (active-plan + captured sets). Red-first `tests/e2e/flight-recorder-replay.spec.js` + deterministic guards in `test/flightRecorderClient.test.js`.
  - ✅ `[trust-critical]` **`CONVO-LOG-1` — FIXED (F09G, 2026-07-17; two focused PRs).** Conversational logging deteriorated around repeated accessory-set messages, a rephrasing/clarification, "Just log it," bare-rep bodyweight knee raises, and Done; the final confirmation was wrong and the owner rejected it. A six-surface map confirmed BOTH parser and conversation-state root causes (independent, split per the card). **Parser slice (#1047):** the knee-raise bodyweight clarification message was hardcoded `"20, 15, 15"` regardless of the actual reps — now echoes the reps actually detected (`services/workoutTextParser.js`; red-first `test/parser-golden.test.js`). **Conversation-state slice:** new module `src/app/pendingClarification.js` HOLDS a `needs_clarification` that already carries detected reps (`err.partialSets`, no longer discarded) and commits **exactly** those sets on a tight affirmation ("Just log it" / "yes" / "log it") through the same `emitSetLogged` buffer path — so no clarified set is dropped, "Just log it" resolves the pending clarification instead of becoming a fabricated set or a session close, and confirmed sets reach the authoritative `getSessionLog()` buffer (retaining all reps + set numbering as bodyweight `0`). Because confirmed sets reach the buffer, the Done→confirmation card is buffer-exact; the empty-buffer Gemini recompile (`runCloseout` → `/api/session/compile` → `compileSessionFromHistory`) only fires when nothing was ever confirmed and stays a preview→approve-gated last resort (never a silent write) — filed below for optional further hardening. Proof: 11 module unit tests + 4 wiring guards (`test/pendingClarification.test.js`) + browser replay (`tests/e2e/pending-clarification-resolve.spec.js`).
  - `[polish]` **`CONVO-CLOSEOUT-RECOMPILE-1`** (F09G follow-up, low risk) — when the session buffer is EMPTY at Done, `runCloseout` recompiles the session from chat history via Gemini (`/api/session/compile` → `compileSessionFromHistory`) and shows it as the confirmation. F09G ensures confirmed conversational sets reach the buffer, so this only fires when nothing was confirmed; and the preview→approve gate backstops it (no silent write). If it is ever observed to invent/drop a set in that empty-buffer branch, make the recompiled result explicitly non-authoritative (label it a reconstruction requiring per-row confirmation) rather than presenting it as the captured session.
  - `[polish]` **`PR-CANDIDATE-LANE-1`** (F09H optional enhancement) — the card allows Atlas to say a PR "appears to be a candidate based on captured sets" before a verified save. Today that grounded framing rides on the LLM prose (already constrained by the set-reaction IRON RULE / register suppressor so it can't claim a permanent PR without `progression_verdict.level === 'new_ground'`). A deterministic client lane that intercepts a PR claim and answers from `progression_verdict` / pending captured sets (candidate-if-unsaved, actual-if-saved) would make it LLM-independent. Not required for correctness; build only if observed prose grounding proves insufficient.
  - `[housekeeping]` **`SIDECAR-DATE-TEST-1`** (Codex advisory on the merged F09I PR #1044) — the F09I route regression tests in `test/api-smoke.test.js` assert `writtenDate === localTodayIso(new Date(), 'America/Vancouver')`, computed at wall-clock time, so **outside** the evening-Pacific window (when the Vancouver and UTC calendar days coincide) the test would still pass even if the route regressed to the old raw `toISOString().slice(0,10)`. The production fix is correct and merged; this is a test-strength gap only. Harden by freezing a boundary instant (e.g. `2026-07-17T05:30:00Z` = `2026-07-16` Vancouver / `2026-07-17` UTC) via a scoped `global.Date` override so the expected local day is known to differ from the raw UTC day. Small standalone test-only follow-up.
  - `[trust-critical]` **`PLAN-LEDGER-1`** — `Session_Plans` stores only headers, not set-level target weight/reps/RIR/set count, so there is no evolving recommendation ledger to compare against actuals or to review at closeout. Owner-approved living coach-plan ledger (two truths: recommendation vs actual; never copy actual backward into plan). → **F10A–F10E** (production Sheet schema application remains owner-reserved).

- 🎨 **Workout Sheet — chat-first shell + pull-down workout surface (owner-directed 2026-07-11; design filed, build awaits owner go).** Owner brief: text-box-driven shell like ChatGPT/Claude (already shipped via composer-first), side drawer for the bells and whistles (already shipped), plus the NEW piece — in workout mode, pull down from the top and the current workout appears as a sheet that **snaps at center and stays**, with **drag-to-reorder** of pending exercises. Design: [`docs/WORKOUT_SHEET_DESIGN.md`](./docs/WORKOUT_SHEET_DESIGN.md); interactive visual spec: `design/mockups/10-workout-sheet.html`. Build queue (tiny PRs, after explicit owner go — PR-1 touches `src/app/app.js` only for the mount point): ✅ **PR-1** `[polish]` sheet shell display-only — **SHIPPED (owner-authorized 2026-07-12).** New module `src/app/workoutSheet.js` (its own file, like `drawer.js`): the pull gesture (session pin = handle, threshold-gated so a tap still toggles the pin; grabber re-arms when open), three detents (closed / center ~58% / full ~92%), snap physics (nearest / flick), rubber-band overpull, dim layer (tap or flick-up / Esc to close), and a read-only done/current/pending card list from the existing canonical selectors (`getActivePlannedSession` / `remainingPlannedExercises` / `getSessionLog`). Workout-mode-only (no active plan → no handle → no sheet). CSP-safe (all transforms/opacity are CSSOM writes) + reduced-motion safe (settle transition disabled). No reorder, no plan mutation, no Sheets/write/engine/GATE-A calls. app.js touched only to expose `getSessionLog` + bump the shell tag; DOM in `index.html`, CSS in `styles.css`, wired via `atlasEntry.js`, precached in `sw.js` (v131→v132). Tests: `test/workoutSheet.test.js` (pure physics + card classification, red-first) + `tests/e2e/workout-sheet.spec.js` (Playwright: pull opens to a detent, cards render, dim closes, no-session gating, reduced-motion). ✅ **PR-2** `[correctness]` drag-to-reorder — **SHIPPED (owner-authorized 2026-07-12).** **Premise correction (verification gate):** the design/brief said "dispatch the EXISTING canonical reorder mutation," but none existed — `activeSession.js` had replace/skip/insert/correctIdentity and `planMutationIntent.js` had no reorder verb. So PR-2 added it engine-first: new pure `reorderExercise(session, ref, toIndex)` in `activeSession.js` (permutes only the pending region; completed/skipped + the current lift are PINNED; exhaustive red-first unit tests in `test/activeSessionState.test.js`). The live order lives in ONE place — `activePlannedSession.exercises` (store.js), which `getCanonicalSession()`/`plannedExerciseOrder()`/`remainingPlannedExercises()` all derive from — so the app.js wrapper `reorderPlannedExercise(fromIndex, toIndex)` permutes that array + re-points the cursor; NO dual-write, NO Session_Plans outcome (a reorder has no outcome vocabulary), NO write path. The sheet holds zero plan logic: PENDING cards carry a ≡ grip (pointer drag) + ▲/▼ controls (non-drag / reduced-motion / a11y fallback), both dispatching the one wrapper; a toast confirms. Equivalence proven in `tests/e2e/workout-sheet-reorder.spec.js` — the drag path yields the SAME `getCanonicalSession()` state as calling `activeSession.reorderExercise` directly (CASE D), the ▲/▼ fallback is identical (CASE E), current/done are pinned (no grip/controls), reduced-motion still reorders (CASE F). `sw.js` v132→v133 + `ATLAS_SHELL_BUILD` matched. **PR-3** `[polish]` header chrome (settings gear, Progress subnav) fully into the drawer, IDs preserved.
  - **PR-3 (or a PR-1 polish) follow-up `[polish]` — center detent hides the sheet header + top cards.** Discovered during PR-2 e2e: the sheet is a fixed ~92vh element translated by `(pos − height)`, so at the center detent (~58%) it slides up ~0.34vh and the visible region is the sheet's LOWER 58% — the header (`TODAY · label` + progress bar) and the first cards (done/current) are above the fold; only later pending cards show. Full detent reveals everything (top at 0). The drag still works (open to full, or drag the visible tail), so this is display-anchoring polish, not a reorder bug. Consider re-anchoring center to reveal header + current (a height/clip model rather than translate of a taller sheet), or documenting center as "the tail at a glance." Out of PR-2's one concern.
  - **PR-2 follow-up `[polish]` (CODEX review, PR #990) — done-card detail on an alias-logged lift.** The sheet's `logSummary` matches logged sets to a card by exact normalized name, but `done` classification upstream resolves aliases (`resolveCompletedIdentity`, e.g. "Dips (Weighted)" → canonical "Weighted Dip"). So an alias-logged lift is correctly marked done but its detail falls back to the generic "logged" instead of "N sets · top …". Degrades gracefully (no crash, no invented number). If PR-2's done-card detail matters, resolve the log-row identity through the same alias ladder before summarizing. Deferred (not authorized): swipe skip/substitute gestures, sheet-initiated substitutions, no-session "recommended plan" sheet variant, haptics. Trust boundaries: the sheet is display + plan-mutation only — no write path, no `write_id`, preview→approve→write untouched.
- 🔎 **2026-07-12 correctness-mission discoveries (filed with the substituted→Done fold-fix PR):**
  - `[correctness]` **Workout Sheet + name-keyed completion vs a duplicate-name plan.** `buildSheetCards` (`src/app/workoutSheet.js`) classifies card status by normalized NAME, so a plan holding the same exercise twice (possible via chat plan-edit `add`; the generator dedupes) renders every same-named slot as `current` together (two ● cards; the later duplicate is pinned — no ≡ grip/▲▼), and the app-wide name-keyed `sessionCompleted` identity flips ALL same-named slots to done after one logged lift. The reorder mutation itself is index-based and duplicate-safe. Real fix is per-slot identity (e.g. `plan_item_id`) in the sheet's status classification — it touches the shared completion-identity model, so it needs its own scoped PR. **Tracked as canonical card F10** (Authoritative planned-slot completion identity) in [`docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`](./docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md).
  - `[polish]` **`reorderPlannedExercise` (`src/app/app.js`) does not call `saveSessionSnapshot()`** — a reorder survives only via the next persist trigger (visibilitychange / next logged set), so a crash/reload right after dragging silently reverts the order. One-line parity with `completePlanItemById`.
  - `[trust-critical]` **Coach-facts hardening residuals (from the effort_verdict engine-rule-bound fix, 2026-07-12).** Two client-echoed coach facts remain enum-bounded but not engine-confirmed: (1) **`rec.deload`** — recomputing needs `readCurrentDeloadState()` (a Deload_State tab read), which the coach route's zero-additional-reads discipline currently forbids; the fork is: accept one small non-hot-path tab read per set reaction, or add a short-TTL cached deload-state read. A forged `in_deload:true` can only change framing (never numbers/writes). (2) **per-block `rec` objects on `kind:'block'`** — each block carries its own client-echoed `effort_verdict`; apply the same last-set recompute per block inside the batch lane (`assembleBatchNoteFacts` callers). (3) **`rec.target_rir` (per #995 review)** — the effort_verdict recompute is engine-rule-bound *relative to the client-supplied target*: a forged `target_rir` still shifts the level (RIR 3 vs a forged target 7 reads "hard"). Fully engine-owning the target trips the SAME fork as (1): an active deload legitimately overrides `target_rir` via the protocol (`index.js` deload prescription), so a server recompute needs the Deload_State read (or cached state) plus the rirPolicy/goal context — resolve (1) and (3) together. Wording-only exposure, auth-gated client; harden one field per PR like the progression_history precedent.

Fixed with per-name exact precedence (`planEditNameEquals`) mirroring the resolvePlanTargets fix; the substring fallback is unchanged for names with no exact slot. Output stays `public/` so `express.static` and every `/app/*` SW `SHELL_ASSETS` URL are byte-stable; `sw.js` untouched (no URL changed → no cache bump). `npm run dev` = Vite dev server (proxy `/api` → backend); `npm run dev:server` = the old `node index.js`.   - ✅ **Follow-up — resolved by PR-22 (2026-07-08), differently than originally sketched, in two layers:** rather than requiring owner confirmation of an unverifiable Render dashboard "Build Command" setting, `public/` regeneration is guaranteed by (1) a `prestart` npm lifecycle hook (`package.json`) — `npm start` always runs `npm run build` first — and (2), since PR review correctly caught that `prestart` alone still depends on an unverifiable dashboard field (the Render *Start Command*, if set to `node index.js` directly, bypasses `prestart` entirely), `index.js::startServer()` now calls `ensurePublicBuilt()` as a last-line-of-defense: builds `public/` itself if still missing, before `app.listen()`. See `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` → "Frontend build".

- ✅ **PR-04 — idempotency survives restarts** `[trust-critical]` (shipped): `services/idempotency.js` now write-through persists `writeRecords` to a single atomic JSON file (env `ATLAS_IDEMPOTENCY_FILE`, default `/tmp/atlas-idempotency.json`), rehydrated lazily on first use with the existing 24h TTL pruned on load and stale `in_progress` records (> 5 min, i.e. abandoned mid-write) downgraded to `failed`/retryable. Persistence is best-effort — any disk error falls back to in-memory with one structured warn and never fails a write; writes are atomic (tmp + rename); public API unchanged; `resetIdempotencyStore()` also unlinks the file. The composite-key sheet dedupe stays the second line of defense (INVARIANTS W1–W3 untouched). New `test/idempotencyPersistence.test.js` covers restart replay, stale-in_progress recovery, TTL prune on load, corrupt-file recovery, persistence-failure fallback, and file cleanup on reset.
  - **Owner infra follow-up (deploy)** `[trust-critical]`: on Render `/tmp` is ephemeral and wiped on redeploy, so the durability only takes effect once the owner attaches a **persistent disk** and sets `ATLAS_IDEMPOTENCY_FILE` to a path on it (e.g. `/var/data/atlas-idempotency.json`). Until then the shield behaves exactly as before (in-memory per process). Infra + env change → owner-gated action; the code default is safe locally.

   - **C3 evidence log — shadow router LIVE (owner flipped `ATLAS_INTENT_ROUTER=shadow` on Render, 2026-07-03)** `[correctness / product-evidence]`: first live classification (from `/api/debug/intent-shadow`): *"My legs are fried right now I think I'll skip single leg press and leg extension"* → `modify_workout`, confidence 0.9, `exclude_exercises` extracted, 745ms off-path. **The live path fumbled the same message**: it fell through to the Gemini chat, which pushed back on the fatigue claim ("your logs actually look recovered") and never removed the exercises — the session pin kept "next: Leg Extension" for the rest of the workout. This is the canonical C3 case — `modify_workout` should route deterministically to the plan-mutation lane. **Promotion stays owner/evidence-gated**: keep collecting shadow entries; when the log shows consistent accuracy, propose the C3 wiring as its own owner-visible decision.

     - **Blocker 1 — session-finish phrases have no home enum (OWNER-RESERVED, not built):** *"I guess I'm done"* / *"Ok sure. We're done."* classify as `clarify_intent` 0.3 because the IntentEnvelope vocabulary (`config/coaching/contracts/intent.vocabulary.json`, 13 intent types) has **no session-lifecycle / finish-session / save / confirmation-card intent**. Adding one is a contract change + new product behavior (`schema_version` bump, downstream Orchestrator handling) → **owner decision required** per the "stop and report the missing enum, do not invent" directive. (Note: *"Where's the session confirmation card?"* is a UI-help question, not a finish intent — `clarify_intent` is defensible for it; the real gap is only the two "done" phrases.) **Decide: add a `finish_session`/`session_lifecycle` intent (and a `help`/`app_question` intent for UI questions?), or leave these as `clarify_intent`.**
     - **Blocker 2 — "It's face pulls next not back squats" → `ok:false` — FIXED:** root cause was a **timeout** (`ms:4004`, at the old 4000ms cap), not a misclassification — the near-identical *"No that was face pulls not back squats"* classified fine (`substitute_exercise` 0.9). Raised `DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS` 4000→6000 (shadow runs fire-and-forget off the reply path, so the budget is invisible to the lifter), added an in-prompt exemplar routing "not Y, X instead / it's X next not Y" plan corrections to `substitute_exercise` with `target_lift`+`exclude_exercises`, and a full-path regression fixture. (Distinct from the separate finding that a PAST-tense *"that was X not Y"* is an identity correction, not a substitution — the router has no correction intent; captured under the C3 evidence log for the promotion decision, not fixed here.)
     - **Blocker 3 — `log_intent` dropped `sets/reps/weight_kg/rir` — EXPECTED, documented:** those are not constraint-vocabulary keys; the IntentEnvelope has no set-detail keys by design (the deterministic slash-parser owns numbers, the router only names the intent), so the model's hallucinated set keys are correctly dropped+recorded. Added a passing regression fixture pinning the behavior + a prompt/gloss note telling the model not to emit set details.
   - ✅ **Live find (owner session 2026-07-03): stated skip fell to the chat LLM — FIXED same day** `[correctness / trust]` — *"My legs are fried right now I think I'll skip single leg press and leg extensions"* never reached the deterministic plan-mutation lane: (1) `classifyMutationIntent` was start-anchored, so a leading REASON clause defeated every pattern; (2) `resolvePlanTargets` couldn't bridge "single leg press" → "Single-Leg Seated Leg Press" (hyphen + extra word beat the substring tiers). Fix (`public/planMutationIntent.js`): a negation- and interrogative-guarded reason-clause fallback (first-person intent marker + mutation verb → re-run the SAME anchored grammar on the extracted clause) and a ≥2-word token-subset match tier. Stated intent now mutates the plan deterministically — pin, composer, and next-up move on; the chat keeps everything that isn't a clear mutation. (The same message is C3 evidence entry #1 — the deterministic lane now covers this case regardless of the router's future.)
   - **Follow-up — `[polish]` #830 review note: add_exercises stacked block shows already-present exercises** — `applyProposedPlanEdit` skips duplicates when adding, but the stacked confirmation block renders ALL of `edit.exercises`, so an exercise the model "adds" that was already in the plan re-renders as if fresh (it is in the plan — redundant, never false). Filter the block to the exercises actually added if it bothers in practice.
   - **Follow-up — `[polish]` #829/#831 review note: word-subset tie-break for same-family duplicate slots** — BOTH subset-tier call sites inherit the ambiguity: `planMutationIntent.resolvePlanTargets` matches every superset slot (half-mitigated by `tryApplyPlanMutation`'s single-phrase `slice(0,1)`), and `app.js resolveCompletedIdentity`'s word-subset tier returns the FIRST matching slot in plan order ("incline press" against Incline Barbell + Incline Dumbbell Press marks the first). Decide one tie-break (e.g. prefer the current/first-pending slot, or refuse and ask) and add a fuzz cell covering both sites if same-family duplicate slots become common.
   - **Follow-up — `[polish]` duplicate-set-group warning at compile time** (carried from the 11:51 live find's "consider" clause — considered in the typo-tolerance PR, deferred as its own concern): when `runCloseout` compiles the buffered session for review, two exercise groups carrying IDENTICAL set signatures (same count + weight/reps/rir per set) where one name is catalog-unknown or fuzzy-matches the other (the Slslp ×4 + Single-Leg Seated Leg Press ×4 shape) should surface a "possible duplicate — same sets under two names" advisory on the review card before save. Touches the review/trust surface (`buildReviewCard`), so it warrants its own focused PR rather than riding the correction-lane diff; the fixed correction lane + check-name chips cover the incident's causal path.
   - ✅ **Live find (owner 2026-07-03, ~1:58pm) — a fresh freestyle log was absorbed into a phantom guided plan (invisible auto-resume) — FIXED (resume-guard PR)** `[correctness/trust]`: the owner logged only "Bench 135 10/2 185 8/2 225 5/2 5/2" into the composer and got full guided-session behavior — a session pin ("Overhead Press · 4 sets in · next: Seated Row" — lifts NOT in the Bench log), a "Bench Press 1 of 5" card, "Moving on — next up: Overhead Press", and a pre-filled "OHP 115 8/2 ×3" placeholder — none of which they engaged. **Root cause:** `restoreSessionSnapshot` (`public/app.js`) resumed a snapshot that carried an `activePlannedSession` but ZERO logged sets (its guard was `!snap.sessionLog.length && !snap.activePlannedSession`), setting `coachSuggestionEngaged = true` so a fresh log slotted into slot 1 of the stale plan — and with `setCount === 0`, `renderResumeNotice` returns early, so the resume was INVISIBLE (no banner, no discard affordance). **Fix (owner "Go", PM authority — derivable from the "freestyle must not auto-guide" principle + the snapshot's stated purpose of protecting unsaved SETS):** resume now requires genuinely logged work — `if (!snap.sessionLog.length) { clearSessionSnapshot(); return false; }`. An engaged-but-unlogged plan no longer auto-reactivates guided mode (it's one tap to re-open the pick); sessions WITH logged sets resume exactly as before (banner + discard intact). Tiny high-risk-file diff, restore path only — no write/trust-loop/proof-field/schema/parser change. Shell v102→v103. Tests: new guard asserted + the old plan-only path asserted gone. **Deferred follow-up `[polish]`:** the session pin's "4 sets in" desync (it labeled the lift "Overhead Press" while counting the just-logged Bench's 4 sets) — a pin label/count mismatch separate from the resume bug; file/fix when the pin renderer is next touched.
   - ✅ **Live bug (owner 2026-07-03, 11:42): chat swap didn't follow through — FIXED** `[correctness]` — after a chat-applied swap to Good Mornings (plan updated, pin correct), the composer placeholder stayed on RDL and a bare "How much weight?" clarified "for which lift?" among ALL plan lifts. Fixes: (1) the `atlas:plan-edit-proposed` listener now fires `announcePlanMutation('', firstUnloggedPlannedLift())` after an applied edit — the SAME signal the deterministic mutation lane uses (empty summary = no extra bubble; composer re-points); (2) `currentLiftFromContext` (`services/sessionQuestionAnswer.js`) treats the FIRST not-completed plan lift as the current lift when `plan_completed` is present — bare shorthand answers from the current step instead of clarifying; legacy clarify stands when `plan_completed` is absent. (The remaining conversational-memory gap — Gemini answering "3×10" without a weight, needing a re-ask — is LLM behavior; the deterministic floor now covers the current-lift facts.)
   - **Follow-up — `[housekeeping]` #833 review note: shorthand done-matching → shared computePlanState gate someday** — `currentLiftFromContext`'s "first not-completed plan lift" check does its own name-based done-matching against `plan_completed`; the server already owns a canonical `computePlanState`. When the answer lane is next touched, route the done-matching through one shared computePlanState-style gate so the composer, next-up router, and shorthand answerer can never disagree on which lift is current.

## Soul Plan S7 / LT-011 reassure voice — live FAIL + deterministic fix, re-validation pending (2026-07-12)

- ✅ **[correctness] Reassure voice was overridden by the challenge signal — FIXED (this PR, `services/coach.js`).** LT-011 live run (agent, 2026-07-12, deployed `99b4af5`) found that an explicit-discouragement chat message returned a **challenge** ("Bench under target 5 of 5 — what's going on?") instead of reassurance, including the multi-clause-negation case ("I don't feel weak but bench is stuck", 3/3 challenge) and a **pain** message (shoulder pain ignored, challenged about bench). Root cause: the mode seam (`deriveChatCoachMode`) correctly returns `reassure` (Owner Decision 1), but `sanitizeChatContext` forwarded `memory_patterns` (the `consistent_underperformance` challenge fuel) to the prompt even in reassure mode, so the model pinned on the pattern. Fix: suppress `memory_patterns` when `coach_mode==='reassure'` (red-first `test/reassureSuppressesChallenge.test.js`). Read-only chat voice — no write-path/schema/trust-loop change.
  - **`[correctness]` watch — no deterministic pain gate on the chat route (resolved behaviorally, kept as a watch).** The chat route deterministically gates only *tiredness* → recovery (`isTirednessExpression`); **pain/injury precedence over reassure is still prompt-instruction-only** (the reassure block's "if the message mentions pain… that takes precedence"). With the challenge fuel removed, that clause now works live — the pain probe led with the pain and protected the joint 3/3. It remains prompt-dependent, not deterministically gated; if a future run shows a pain message not prioritized, add a deterministic pain/safety gate on the chat path (mirroring the tiredness→recovery gate) as its own scoped PR — do not fold it into other work.

## Deep-review audit — 2026-07-07 — open findings

Full evidence, failure scenarios, and fix sketches: [`docs/AUDIT_2026-07-07_DEEP_REVIEW.md`](./docs/AUDIT_2026-07-07_DEEP_REVIEW.md). One finding = one future fix PR (or one named test in the remediation PR that restructures its code). Verdicts: **E** = confirmed empirically, **C** = confirmed by code-read, **P** = plausible (verify-first).

**P0 — data-integrity (historical severity tag; sequencing now lives in the V1 execution plan, not the retired PR-08 ordering):**

- [x] **PARSE-2** `[trust-critical]` **(E)** — ✅ **FIXED (PR-0F, `claude/atlas-remediation-v2-phase-0-yxfp1t`)**: in `parseMultilineLogSets`, a digit-free `missing_sets` line is now re-parsed standalone (`activeExercise: null`) before being accepted as a header — it becomes a header only if it names its OWN exercise. An unknown line like `rear delt machine` (which only carried the previous lift via context) is instead surfaced as `unresolved` and `carryExercise` is reset to null, so following bare set lines dead-end to an ask instead of merging. `bench 225 5/2\nrear delt machine\n95 12/2` → Bench Press logs `[[225,5,2]]` only; the machine line + its 95×12 surface unresolved, never merged. Legitimate name headers (`Bench`⏎`225 5/2`) and continuation lines (`bench 225 5/2`⏎`205 5/3`) still resolve. Golden pins in `test/parser-golden.test.js` (PARSE-2 block); full suite green. *(was: the digit-free line became a phantom header of the previous lift — `workoutTextParser.js:647-654,786-792`.)*
  - **Follow-up `[polish]` (PR-0F review note):** the standalone re-parse in the header branch would dead-end a lone **contextual-alias** sub-header (`lats`/`incline`) whose standalone parse returns `ambiguous_exercise_alias` rather than `missing_sets`. Verified this does NOT regress realistic inputs — the whole-text collapse / multi-exercise split resolve `lat pulldown 130 8/2 ⏎ lats ⏎ 120 10/2` (and variants) before the rescue ever sees `lats` as a standalone header (pinned in the PARSE-2 guard golden test). If a future input ever routes a contextual-alias sub-header through the rescue, it dead-ends to an ask (trust-safe, never mislogs); to make that path keep merging, also accept a standalone whose `partial.exercise === carryExercise` in the header branch.
- [x] **PARSE-3** `[trust-critical]` **(E)** — ✅ **FIXED (PR-0G, `claude/atlas-remediation-v2-phase-0-yxfp1t`)**: the bare-pair branch now refuses (returns an `AMBIGUOUS_SET_FORMAT` sentinel that `parseSetGroups` propagates — distinct from the `xN>10` null which stays `missing_sets` — so the whole parse dead-ends to a `needs_clarification` with the `ambiguous_set_format` warning asking for slash notation) when a candidate `{weight} {reps}` pair is implausible as a load — `weight < 6` (an RIR token mis-read as a weight) or `reps > 50` (a weight mis-read as reps). `bench 225 5 2 185 8 2` and `squat 315 5 1 275 8` now ask instead of emitting a 2 lb × 185-rep phantom; the warm-up climb (`140 15 190 10`), single triple (`225 5 2`, whole-text anchor), light bare pairs (`15 20`), and slash notation are untouched. Null threaded through `parseWithExercise` / `parseUnknownExercise` / the Dips bodyweight check. Golden pins in `test/parser-golden.test.js` (PARSE-3 block); full suite green. *(was: `bench 225 5 2 185 8 2` → a 2 lb × 185-rep phantom, RIRs lost — `workoutTextParser.js:1247-1254`.)*
  - **Follow-up `[polish]` (PR-0G review notes, both trust-safe — asks, never mislogs):** (1) `MIN_PLAUSIBLE_WEIGHT = 6` also refuses a legit **multi-set** bare pair whose load is ≤ 5 lb (e.g. `curls 5 12 5 15` — real 5 lb DB work) because in the bare grammar a 5 lb weight is indistinguishable from an RIR token; single ≤5 lb sets stay safe via the whole-text anchor, so impact is narrow (a clarification, not a mislog). This is wider than the 6–19 / 20–49 residual gap the code comment names. (2) For a leading **unknown** name with an implausible tail (`zzq 2 185 8 2`), the refusal routes through `parseUnknownExercise → null` to the generic `missing_exercise` ask rather than the specific `ambiguous_set_format` slash-notation message — trust-safe, just less precise copy. Both resolve cleanly once PR-14 makes the parser catalog-aware (real weights vs RIR become distinguishable); no code change taken here to keep PR-0G tightly scoped.

### PR-10 live-session regression addendum — disposition (2026-07-08)

Source: [`docs/PR10_REGRESSION_ADDENDUM.md`](./docs/PR10_REGRESSION_ADDENDUM.md) (owner-captured v116 session). PR-10 shipped the **structural single state store** (`src/app/store.js` — session/plan slice: `activePlannedSession`, `sessionChromeExpanded`, `coachSuggestionEngaged`, `pendingSubstitution`, `sessionLog`, `sessionCompleted`, `sessionSavedLog`) + **SESS-2** (snapshot pendingSubstitution). The addendum's remaining seven cases are **behaviour fixes in coach / suggestion / correction / recap layers**, not state-store ownership; per the addendum's own clause they are dispositioned here (**owner ratification requested**) rather than folded into the structural migration, which the Minefield Map freezes as "relocate, don't change." The store is the foundation each fix now reads from.

- [ ] **ADD-3** `[correctness]` `[new-capability]` — **"stay away from legs and lower back" must constrain remaining suggestions** for the session. Session-scoped constraint applied to substitution/suggestion routing — a **new capability**, not a bug fix (Atlas's constraint system is persistent-only today; no ephemeral session-scoped avoidance). **Disposition (2026-07-08):** owner-reserved (Escalation Policy v3 criterion 2 — new product scope / coaching behavior); owner chose **spec-to-Decision-Desk** over building silently. **Spec + options filed in Atlas Decision Desk issue #914** (session-scoped-ephemeral [recommended] vs reuse-persistent-constraints vs coach-voice-only; region→muscle/pattern map via `analytics.classifyMuscleGroup`; filter in `checkAndSuggestSubstitute` / `/api/suggest-substitute`). **Awaiting desk verdict** before any build. The other five addendum ADDs (1/2/4/5/6) are shipped.
- [x] **ADD-4** `[correctness]` — ✅ **FIXED (this PR, `claude/add-4-substitute-satisfies-slot`)**. Verification gate first: the single-slot case already reconciled via the existing substring identity tier; the bug reproduces only when BOTH the suggested base (`Dumbbell Flyes`) and the logged variant (`Incline Dumbbell Flyes`) exist as slots and only the variant is logged — the base was stranded as "still remaining". Fixed at the **read-only recap layer**: new pure `reconcileSubstitutedRemaining(completed, remaining)` in `src/app/activeSession.js` drops a remaining slot when a completed exercise is a strict, word-anchored **superset variant** of it (`"<modifier> <slot>"` — `Incline Dumbbell Flyes` satisfies `Dumbbell Flyes`; `Barbell Row` satisfies `Row`). **Directional** so a base never satisfies a more-specific slot (flat `Bench Press` never completes an `Incline Bench Press` slot); word-boundary-anchored so it never fires mid-word (`Leg Express` ≠ `press`); and **qualifier-gated** (PR #911 review) so a *distinct named movement* that merely ends with a base's phrase does not false-satisfy it — the leading modifier words must all be recognized equipment/angle/grip qualifiers (`Incline Dumbbell`, `Barbell`, `Close-Grip`), so `Romanian Deadlift`/`Sumo Deadlift`/`Upright Row` never drop a `Deadlift`/`Row` slot (which would hide genuinely-undone work). Applied only in `canonicalSessionRecap` (`src/app/app.js`) — **no write-path / save-payload / session-state change** (the written rows still come from `sessionLog`). Tests in `test/activeSessionState.test.js` (pure edge cases + an end-to-end canonical-session repro). Frozen-exports guard updated deliberately. Full suite green (4713).
- **Follow-up `[polish]` (from PR #910 review, ADD-2):** the `"no X"` negation-skip maps *defer* and *remove* to the same effect — replying `"no rdls"` to "ready for RDLs?" skips the slot even if the lifter meant to defer, not remove it. Acceptable today (owner-driven mapping, downstream plan-slot gate keeps it conservative); revisit if a genuine "defer vs. skip" case surfaces.
- [x] **ADD-5** `[trust-critical]` — ✅ **FIXED (this PR, `claude/workout-presentation-consistency-nhifc4`)**: a demonstrative correction (**"I meant incline dumbbell flyes"**) no longer silently relabels a completed lift it does not clearly refer to. `tryApplyIdentityCorrection` (`src/app/app.js`) now guards the `{to}`-only (that-was/i-meant) form: it applies the relabel to the tail lift **only when that lift is still the focus** — refusing (and asking via a new read-only `atlas:identity-correction-ambiguous` event, rendered by `coach-conversation.js`) when the corrected identity is already **its own logged group** OR the **session has moved off the just-logged lift**. The move-off signal is a durable `coachDiscussionSinceLog` flag set by the discuss→coach route and reset in `emitSetLogged` when a set is logged — it survives the route (unlike `activeExercise`, which the coach route nulls, so it could not carry the headline "discussed-but-not-logged fly" repro; that gap was caught in review and fixed). The completed lift is left exactly as logged — no permanent-record corruption. The `"X is Y"` (`intent.from`) user-named form is unchanged. Live bug reproduced by failing tests first, then green: 5 new cases in `test/identityCorrectionWiring.test.js` (Bench protected from a later fly correction; earlier completed lift never chosen by word overlap; completed-lift relabel after focus-moved-on asks; target-already-logged asks; the legitimate just-logged re-identification still relabels). Full suite green (4700). **Scope:** ADD-5 only — **ADD-2, ADD-4, ADD-6 remain OPEN** (below) for follow-up PRs.
- [x] **ADD-6** `[correctness]` — ✅ **PRIMARY INVARIANT SATISFIED + REGRESSION-LOCKED (this PR, `claude/add-6-recap-reconcile`)**. Verification gate: the load-bearing requirement — *"the recap must not show completed/substituted exercises as still remaining"* — is **already delivered** by **ADD-2** (an explicit skip splices the slot out of the plan, so it's gone from `remaining`) + **ADD-4** (`reconcileSubstitutedRemaining` — a logged variant satisfies its base slot). Empirically confirmed for both §6 shapes (fly-off-plan and fly-was-a-slot): `remaining = [Curls]` (only the genuinely-undone lift). This PR **locks that composition** with regression tests in `test/activeSessionState.test.js` covering the full §6 live shape (OHP/RDL-skipped/Bench/Seated Row/Face Pull/Curls + added Incline Dumbbell Flyes) — asserting no logged/substituted lift ever appears as remaining and a canonically-skipped slot never resurfaces. Test-only; no runtime change.
  - **Follow-up `[polish]` — positively SURFACE skipped lifts in the recap copy ("Skipped: RDL").** §6 also asks the recap to *include* the skipped RDL by name. Currently `skipPlannedExercise` **splices** the slot (its own note: *"Representing a skipped slot as skipped (vs absent) is deferred to 2b"*), so the skip's *effect* is correct (never shown as remaining) but the lift isn't named as skipped. Deferred as its own scoped PR (spreads high-risk `app.js`): needs a session-scoped `sessionSkipped` list recorded at the **explicit-skip** chokepoints only (`tryApplyPlanMutation` skip/compound-extra paths — NOT the auto-advance skip in `advancePlannedSession`), a `skipped` field on `canonicalSessionRecap` (excluding any later-logged lift), and a "Skipped: …" line in the coach recap intro (`coach-conversation.js`). Read-only/narration — no write-path or save-payload effect.

## Atlas Flight Recorder — replayable real-session transcript (owner-approved design 2026-07-05)

Design spec: [`docs/FLIGHT_RECORDER_SPEC.md`](./docs/FLIGHT_RECORDER_SPEC.md). An **optional, feature-flagged (`ATLAS_FLIGHT_RECORDER=1`, default OFF), append-only** logging system that records the **user-visible app experience + decision trail** during real sessions, so a developer can **replay** what happened instead of debugging by screenshots and pasted JSON. It answers: *"what did the user do, what did Atlas think, what did Atlas decide, and what exactly did Atlas show?"*. Owner/debug telemetry — NOT training data: writes only to a new optional `Flight_Recorder` tab, never `Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`/`Modality_Log`, no `write_id`, never routes through the preview→approve→write trust loop. Best-effort + TOTAL (a recorder failure can never surface a user error, block the UI, retry-storm, or interrupt logging). Owner decisions (2026-07-05): JSON-in-cells MVP schema; batching = flush at 25 events / every 10s / immediately on error·bug_marker·pagehide; full `correlation_id` excluded from initial scope (→ FR5).

Build as tiny PRs, one concern each:
- ✅ **PR-FR2 — server-side API-flow recording + read route** `[correctness]` — **shipped (PR #856).** `recordApiFlow` (`services/flightRecorder.js`) builds one `api_response` event per served request, rings it, and best-effort appends one row via the standard `sheets.appendRows` — so it targets the **server's own `GOOGLE_SHEETS_ID`** (sandbox server → sandbox `Flight_Recorder`; production → production; no second sheet target). A thin **observe-only middleware** in `index.js` (flag-gated, skips `/api/flight/*`, records on `res.on('finish')`, never reads/mutates the response, never blocks) captures **both real app traffic and the simulation harness** (an HTTP client driving the server). `GET /api/flight/recent` serves the in-memory ring. **Isolation:** the sim harness marks requests `x-atlas-simulation: 1`, and `recordApiFlow` never persists sim-marked traffic to a non-sandbox sheet (ring-only) — so a sim can never write into the production `Flight_Recorder`, on top of sim write-mode already refusing non-sandbox servers. **`request_summary` is SHAPE-only** (top-level keys + size, never field values), so workout content never lands in the debug tab (owner decision 2026-07-05, honoring the "prefer summaries for large JSON" guardrail; from review #856 feedback). Proofs: `test/flightRecorderApiFlow.test.js` (flag-off=zero writes; sim→non-sandbox=no write / sim→sandbox=write / real→any=write; redaction; TOTAL) + `test/api-smoke.test.js` end-to-end (flag gate, `Flight_Recorder`-only, sim isolation) + a harness sim-header proof. No `Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`/trust-tab write, no proof-field/trust-loop/parser change; flag default-OFF → byte-identical.
  - Deferred `[housekeeping]`: server-side **batching** (currently one best-effort append per API request, consistent with the shadow-lane volume and fine for a debug window; batch if a high-traffic observation window makes the append count matter).
- ✅ **PR-FR3 — frontend capture + batching + client ingest** `[correctness]` — **shipped (this PR).** New self-contained `public/flightRecorder.js` (UMD, never throws) that attaches its OWN listeners — capture-phase clicks (`user_action`), composer `#logger-form` submit (`user_input`), the existing `atlas:*` CustomEvents (`preview-ready`/`chat-message`/`set-logged`/`plan-mutated`/… → `card_rendered`/`coach_message_rendered`/`session_state_changed`), `pagehide`/`visibilitychange` — and reads the DOM for `ui_snapshot`/`session_state`, so it needs **NO edit to the restricted `public/app.js`** (only the standard shell-version bump v107→v108 + SW precache). It self-activates ONLY when `GET /api/flight/recent` reports the flag on (else fully inert); batched flush (25 events / 10s / on error·bug_marker / `pagehide` via `fetch` keepalive so the auth header survives). `POST /api/flight/ingest` (writeCapable, rate-limited) → `recordClientBatch` appends the whole batch in ONE best-effort call to `Flight_Recorder` (never a workout/trust tab), **202 no-op when the flag is off**, same sim→non-sandbox isolation guard. Proofs: `test/flightRecorderClient.test.js` + `test/api-smoke.test.js` ingest end-to-end. Full suite green. No trust-tab write, no proof-field/trust-loop/parser change (the only `public/app.js` touch is the version const). **Review #857 fixes:** `/api/flight/ingest` gets its OWN `flight_ingest` rate-limiter bucket (generous default) so best-effort telemetry can NEVER draw from the trust-write budget and 429 a real set-log/undo; the client also captures JS `error`/`unhandledrejection` events (makes the taxonomy's `error` type + the immediate-flush branch live); `recordClientBatch` catch reports the real flag state.
  - **Deferred `[correctness]` — shadow-linkage enrichment (`_flight.shadow`)**: additive response-envelope block (Brain/Intent created/route/count) the client stamps onto its event; deferred from FR3 to keep that slice to capture + ingest. Small follow-up.
- **PR-FR5 (owner-gated) — full `correlation_id`** `[trust-critical]`: mint a per-request id and append it as a trailing column to `Brain_Shadow` + `Intent_Shadow` (append-only schema change → owner approval) so Flight_Recorder ↔ shadow tabs join on one exact key. Deferred by owner (2026-07-05).
- **Flight-test triage 2026-07-06 (independent telemetry review)** — ran the extractor against the live workbook's latest cycle (single ~20-min session, 8 `bug_marker`s). **Shipped fixes:** "over head press" spacing alias (PR #869); unknown-lift log survives conversational filler before its sets — "Curls I did 35 12/2 x3 actually" (PR #870); a feedback opening line no longer drops the following log line — "You missed curls" (PR #871). **Deferred / uncertain (NOT built — need owner or more scope):**
  - `[correctness]` **Feedback line → coach routing**: after PR #871, an opening feedback line ("weight you suggested is too light") is surfaced as an `unresolved` line while the log is recovered. Routing that feedback to the coach as a modify/clarify signal (so Atlas *responds* to it, not just logs past it) is a larger interaction concern — build as its own PR.
  - `[correctness]` **"I'll skip RDLs today" not honored**: a natural-language skip mixed with a log ("I'll skip RDLs today\n\nBench …") parses the Bench fine but drops the skip into `unresolved_lines`; the plan still lists RDL as pending (marker "Alta's got tripped up"). Honoring an inline skip = a plan-mutation intent (feature), not a tiny parser fix.
  - **coaching-philosophy → owner-reserved** (not code bugs): OHP "only two working sets" (marker seq18 — a *question*; engine prescribed 1 warm-up + 2 working by design; at most a transparency gap); coach commentary appearing on a plain set log (marker "Not coaches note here again").
  - `[correctness]` **recheck after #870/#871**: the session recap listed already-logged lifts as "still on your plan" and the curls coaching pulled a ~105–116 lb working range (Overhead Press's, not curls') — both partly downstream of the two parse drops now fixed; re-verify on the next live cycle before opening a PR. Also low-pri: `log-modality` 422 on "You missed curls" (likely correct validation).

**Recommendation (owner-approved):** build the FR1–FR3 vertical slice before further simulator expansion — the simulator can't reproduce real-device UI state the owner is currently screenshotting.

## QA campaign — 2026-06-26 live gym + composer playtest (B1–B10)

Ordered remediation of the findings in [`docs/UX_PLAYTEST_2026-06-26_LIVE_GYM_AND_COMPOSER_SESSION.md`](./docs/UX_PLAYTEST_2026-06-26_LIVE_GYM_AND_COMPOSER_SESSION.md) (the source-of-truth record; §2 = the B1–B10 findings, §5 = the follow-up PR split). One concern per PR, in priority order. (The "model hold point between PRs" cadence is a historical note — no builder model is mandatory; see the umbrella disclaimer at the top of this file.)

- ✅ **B4 — Freestyle logging must not auto-guide ("next up")** `[correctness]` (this PR) — **Verdict: BROKEN → fixed.** Root cause: `handleSetLogged` in `public/coach-conversation.js` always fell back to `getNextExerciseInPlan()` (→ `/api/plan/today`) when `detail.nextPlanned` was null, even during freestyle logging with an empty `plannedOrder`. Fix: gate the fallback on `hasEngagedPlan = (detail.plannedOrder || []).length > 0` — freestyle (empty `plannedOrder`) now yields `nextEx = null` immediately, so no "Moving on — next up: X" appears. Guided sessions (engaged `activePlannedSession` or `coachSuggestionEngaged`) still use the fallback when needed. The `inEngagedPlan` second-line guard and the duplicate-resurrection guard both remain. Tests: `test/freestyleNextUp.test.js` (5 structural cases) + updated assertion in `test/unit.test.js`.
  - **Deferred — `compactPrescription` double-normalization drops weight/reps for `activePlannedSession` entries** `[polish]`: `nextUpPlaceholderFromPlan` (`public/coach-conversation.js`) always returns `null` for entries from `activePlannedSession` because `compactPrescription` calls `normalizePlanExercise` on an **already-normalized** entry. Normalized entries carry `name`/`liftCode`/`weight`/`reps`/`sets`/`rir` but not `exercise`/`target_weight`/`target_reps` (the raw intent fields), so the second `normalizePlanExercise` call resolves `name='LPD01'` (from `liftCode`) and `weight=null`/`reps=null` → `compactPrescription` returns `null`. The engaged-session composer placeholder falls through to `formatNextPlaceholder` via `/api/plan/today` (expanded per-set format), or bare name if that call misses. Fix: either gate `compactPrescription` to skip the `normalizePlanExercise` call when the input is already normalized (has a `name`/`weight`/`reps` shape), or read `activePlannedSession` entries directly in `nextUpPlaceholderFromPlan`. No trust-loop / write-path / parser change — display only. File when the owner wants the compact `xN` placeholder in guided sessions (e.g. "Lat Pulldown 170 8/2 x3" vs the current expanded "Lat Pulldown 170 8/2 8/2 8/2").
---

## Coaching reactions — 2026-07-11 (every logged lift gets one concise reaction; history-aware facts)

- ✅ **[correctness] `ack_only` rendered SILENCE — a routine on-plan lift got only the receipt card, no coaching reaction** — **shipped (PR 1 of 2).** Every logged lift must receive ONE concise coaching reaction; `ack_only` means a BRIEF acknowledgement, not silence (design intent in `docs/ATLAS_CONVERSATION_PROTOTYPE_V1_PLAN.md`: "✅ Bench logged."). Root cause: `getInWorkoutNote` (`src/app/coach-conversation.js`) returned `note: null` for a `note_tier==='ack_only'` block, so the caller typed nothing. Fix: return a deterministic `coachVoiceTemplates.templatedAckLine()` (name-free — the readback card names the lift and the multi-lift caller prefixes attribution; number-free — the sets live on the card), still flagged `ack_only:true` so the "Next time:" box + separate effort line stay suppressed (routine = minimal). The existing multi-lift loop (one `getInWorkoutNote` per lift) + the session-level next-exercise handoff already run after it, so every lift is coached and the handoff follows. Client rendering only — deterministic, survives an LLM outage (the server already skips Gemini for a routine block); no Sheets/GATE A/trigger, no write-path/proof-field/parser change. Tests: `test/coachConversation.test.js` (templatedAckLine concise/name-free/number-free + the ack_only branch returns a brief note, not `note:null`); `test/unit.test.js` (contract updated silence→brief-ack, Next-box suppression retained).
  - **Deferred `[polish]` — de-templated ack variety.** `templatedAckLine()` is one fixed line for now (owner: "keep wording simple for now"). When richer voice is wanted, add a few rotating phrasings (like `VERDICT_VARIANTS`) so back-to-back routine lifts don't read identically — owner-gated coach-wording polish, not a correctness gap.
- ✅ **[correctness] PR 2 — history-aware coaching facts — shipped.** A per-lift `progression_history` fact now reaches the coach: **current verdict** + **previous verdict** (band verdicts from `analytics.progressionVerdict`/`progressionBand` — today's top vs the lifter's own recent band, and the prior session's top vs the band before it), **consecutive on-target sessions** + the **next engine-authorized progression checkpoint** (both from the wired `rules/progressionRules.holdUntilClean` — its clean-session count and `hold`/`load` decision, surfaced verbatim). New pure composer `services/progressionHistory.js` (`buildProgressionHistory(rows, liftCode)`) reuses those existing rules ONLY — no invented thresholds, no re-derived numbers (the engine's `criterion_progress` string is parsed for its own counts). Computed SERVER-SIDE in `enrichCoachFacts` (`services/liveIntelligence.js`) from the lift-restricted log (no client influence), whitelisted via `sanitizeProgressionHistory` in `services/coach.js` (enum-checks the verdict levels + checkpoint decision — a hallucinated level/decision is dropped), and worded by a new coach-prompt rule that states the arc + checkpoint but **never authorizes a load increase the engine hasn't** (`decision` must be `load`). Wording kept simple per owner. No Sheets/GATE A/trigger, no write-path/proof-field/parser/schema change. Tests: `test/progressionHistory.test.js` (the four facts cross-checked against the real engine functions), `test/coach.test.js` (whitelist forward + enum drop + prompt rule), `test/liveIntelligence.test.js` (server-side attach).
  - **Deferred `[polish]` — richer arc wording + a strict tail-consecutive streak (CODEX review note, PR #988).** `consecutive_on_target` is `holdUntilClean`'s clean-session COUNT at the current load (the engine's operative on-target count toward the next jump — it increments on ANY clean session, so `clean→dirty→clean` reads as 2), NOT a strict tail-streak over the band verdict. The field keeps the owner's requested name; the coach prompt now explicitly forbids the model from asserting the sessions were "back-to-back"/"in a row" (truthfulness guard) and the model anchors to `criterion_progress` ("X of Y clean sessions at Z", which never says "consecutive"). If the owner wants a genuinely strict consecutive-verdict streak (a second on-target definition) or de-templated arc phrasings, that's a follow-up.

## Bug remediation — 2026-07-11 (explicit substitution on the last pending slot did nothing, coach falsely said "Plan updated")

- ✅ **[correctness] "Swap leg extensions out for something else." left Leg Extension in place and Atlas said "Plan updated"** — **shipped (this PR).** Fixture: RDL, Back Squat, Single-Leg Seated Leg Press and Seated Row completed, **Leg Extension the only pending slot**, header/cursor still stale on Seated Row. **Root cause:** `recommendSubstitute('Leg Extension')` returned `null` — Leg Extension (quad knee-isolation) had **no `SUBSTITUTE_CATALOG` entry** — so the deterministic implicit-substitution lane (`tryApplyImplicitSubstitution` in `src/app/app.js`, via read-only `/api/suggest-substitute`) fell through, and the message reached the LLM chat, which claimed "Plan updated" without swapping. The other three diagnosed facets were already correct and are now regression-locked: **target resolution** resolves the NAMED pending slot from the canonical session's pending status (cursor-independent, so the stale Seated Row cursor is irrelevant); **active-index repair** advances the forward-only `syncPlannedIndexToCanonical` cursor onto the substitute once the swap lands; **confirmation truthfulness** — the deterministic lane only announces `Swapped X → Y` on a real change (`applySessionSubstitution` returns false on a no-op) and the LLM `applyProposedPlanEdit` path already gates "Plan updated." on a truthful `applied`. **Fix (smallest):** add `'Leg Extension': ['Leg Press', 'Hack Squat', 'Goblet Squat']` to `RAW_CATALOG` (`services/substitutionRecommender.js`) — quad-region squat-pattern movements, the honest best (no same-pattern quad isolation scores excellent; Leg Curl is the hamstring antagonist → correctly graded `poor` and never surfaced). Deterministic engine + data only; no Sheets/scored-progression/GATE A/trigger change, no write-path/trust-loop/proof-field/parser change. Tests (red-first, verified failing without the catalog line): unit — `test/substitutionRecommender.test.js` (Leg Extension → acceptable quad sub, never Leg Curl, context-aware avoidance); live path — `test/api-smoke.test.js` (`/api/suggest-substitute` Leg Extension intent:substitute → valid sub); E2E wiring — `test/legExtensionSubstitutionWiring.test.js` (the exact fixture with the stale cursor + real recommender: in-place replace, substitute becomes active, one truthful `Swapped Leg Extension → …` announcement, skip + named-swap controls unregressed).
  - **Deferred — the malformed "India want…" → unrelated Bench Press response** `[correctness]` (kept separate per this task's scope): a malformed/garbled composer input beginning "India want…" produced an unrelated Bench Press coaching reply. Not addressed here (out of the substitution concern). Investigate whether the input mis-routes through the substitute/coach lane (mis-parse of "want" or a fuzzy exercise match) and where the confident-but-wrong Bench Press answer originates; file the repro when reproduced. Own PR.
  - **Deferred — sibling isolation families still have no `SUBSTITUTE_CATALOG` entry** `[correctness]`: this PR adds only Leg Extension (the reported slot). The identical gap remains for other common isolations — **Leg Curl** (hamstring; its acceptable subs are posterior-chain hinge movements — RDL / Good Morning — a different candidate set and reasoning than Leg Extension), plus arm/delt/calf isolations (Bicep Curl, Tricep Extension, Lateral Raise, Calf Raise) — so an explicit "swap X out for something else" on any of them still falls through to the LLM. Extend the catalog family-by-family in a future authorized PR (each family is its own small concern with its own quality-vetted candidates).

## Bug remediation — 2026-07-02 owner live re-test (recovery-session scold, 2nd recurrence of BUG-20260629-204817)

- ✅ **[correctness] Recovery/Pump session scolds the prescribed light loads — "below your working range / add weight / Room to progress"** — **shipped (this PR).** Owner screenshots 07-02: a Recovery/Pump Coach's Pick prescribed 55×15@RIR4 / 90×12@RIR5; logging those exact sets produced "That was a light session… below your working range of 60–70… get back in the groove", "You're right on track to add weight next session", and a "Next time: Room to progress — move to 60 × 15" card. **Two root gaps left by the -204817 fix:** (1) `GET /api/recommend/next/:liftCode` (`index.js`) never read the `?intentId` query param the client has sent since that fix — so `recommendNextSet`'s recovery branch and recovery target-RIR were dead in production (normal-day target RIR 3 → RIR-5 sets read `far_easy` → add-load card + polluted `rec` facts fed to the coach LLM). (2) The prescribed intent never reached the LLM facts — `sanitizeFacts` (`services/coach.js`) had no field for it, and the prompt's recovery override keyed only off an active deload / convergence advisory, so the note worded `under_shot`/`far_easy` as a scold. **Fix:** route passes a whitelisted `intentId` (`recovery_pump`/`deload_reset` only — other intents byte-identical) into `recommendNextSet`; `sanitizeFacts` forwards `session_intent` (same whitelist) and `buildCoachSystemPrompt` frames a recovery session's light/high-RIR work as ON-PLAN by design (overrides the add-weight steer, forbids "get back in the groove"-style scolds). Read-only paths — no write/trust-loop/proof-field/schema/parser change. Tests: route control/recovery/junk-intent smoke (`test/api-smoke.test.js`), `session_intent` whitelist + prompt rule (`test/coach.test.js`).
  - **Deferred — intent-aware target RIR for NON-recovery intents at the route** `[correctness]`: the `?intentId` passthrough is deliberately whitelisted to the two recovery objectives (smallest safe fix for the live bug). `recommendedTargetRir` supports every intent (`build_strength` → RIR 2, `build_muscle` accessory → 1, etc.), and the client already sends whatever intent is active — but the route still ignores non-recovery intents, so e.g. a strength session's effort verdict is judged against the general-health default target. Wiring the full intent set changes effort verdicts on normal days; do it as its own reviewed PR with fixture coverage per goal.
  - **Deferred — centralize the recovery-intent list** `[housekeeping]` (Codex review PR #788, non-blocking): `['recovery_pump', 'deload_reset']` is now duplicated across four sites — the `/api/recommend/next` route (`index.js`), `computeSetEffortExtras` (`index.js`), `RECOVERY_SESSION_INTENTS` (`services/coach.js`), and `recommendNextSet` (`services/analytics.js`) — coupled only by "keep in lockstep" comments. If a third recovery intent is ever added to `INTENT_TO_GOAL` (`services/rirPolicy.js`) without mirroring, the voices diverge silently. Export one shared constant (natural home: `services/rirPolicy.js`, e.g. derived from `INTENT_TO_GOAL` entries mapping to `recovery`) and consume it at all four sites in a future authorized touch of those files.

## Bug remediation — 2026-07-02 owner report (freestyle shorthand silently dropped)

- ✅ **[correctness] "Lat pull 175 10/2 x3" typed after Bench Press silently dropped instead of logged** — **shipped (this PR).** Owner screenshots 07-02 (freestyle session, no active plan): after logging Bench Press then Hammer Curl, "Lat pull 175 10/2 x3" got only "Noted — keep logging and say 'log it' when you're done" — no confirmation card, and the set was absent from the final session summary (2 exercises / 8 sets, missing Lat Pulldown entirely). **Root cause:** "lat pull" is a `CONTEXTUAL_ALIASES` entry for Lat Pulldown (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) that only auto-resolves when `context.activeExercise` already equals "Lat Pulldown" (cross-turn continuation); with a mismatched active exercise (Bench Press) it correctly falls to `needs_clarification` ("Did you mean Lat Pulldown?") but that response omitted the `partial.exercise` field the other `needs_clarification` branches in the same file set. The frontend's trust-gap guard (`public/app.js` — `err.parsedIntent === 'needs_clarification' && err.recognizedExercise`, added to stop exactly this class of silent drop) reads `parsed.partial.exercise` into `err.recognizedExercise`; with it null, the guard didn't fire and the typed set fell through to `routeMessageToCoach` → the generic numeric-text `chatFallback` reply, discarding the set with no trace. **Fix (`services/workoutTextParser.js`, additive-only):** the contextual-alias-mismatch `needs_clarification` return now carries `partial: { exercise, raw_name }` like its sibling branches, so the existing frontend guard surfaces "Did you mean Lat Pulldown? Use the full exercise name to be sure." as an actionable status message instead of a misleading "Noted" chat reply. No parser-grammar/alias-resolution change (contextual aliases still require the strong alias or a matching active exercise to auto-log — that scope stays as-is; see the related deferred shorthand items below), no write-path/schema/trust-loop change. Tests: `test/unit.test.js` (`contextual_alias_mismatch_carries_partial_exercise_so_it_is_not_silently_dropped`). Full suite green.
  - **Deferred — freestyle sessions still can't auto-log bare shorthand ("Lat pull") without the full name** `[polish]`: this PR closes the *silent-drop* trust gap (the lifter is now told to retype with the full name) but does not make "lat pull"/"incline" themselves loggable in a freestyle session with no matching active exercise — `rowsFromUnresolvedPlannedLead` only bridges shorthand when a **planned session** has that lift pending (see the "Still open" notes on the #480/#481 entries above). Promoting `lat pull` from a contextual to a strong alias (or a freestyle-aware bridge) is a slash-notation/parser-grammar contract change and stays owner-gated per `CLAUDE.md`.

## Bug remediation — 2026-07-02 owner live test (B5 date regression on the conversation surface)

- 🔴 **CONFIRMED lost-sets (owner answer + retest IMG_5441/5442, 07-02 7:51): the five pasted exercises were NEVER saved.** The first paste got a fluent LLM readback ("I've got Bench Press 135 × 10 reps @ RIR 5 … what's your main goal for this session?") that *sounded* logged but buffered nothing; "Done" produced nothing; an expanded re-paste (one set per line) hit the generic coach fallback ("Noted — keep logging and say 'log it'"); "Log it" then failed with **"Couldn't find any sets in the conversation."** Five exercises acknowledged twice, zero rows buffered or saved — the trust path silently discarded the input and the LLM readback actively masked it. This is the known **Multi-workout composer bug (1)** re-confirmed for a third paste shape — see the 2026-07-02 re-test bullet added to that section below. **That lane is now the top bug priority, ahead of the B5 date fix.** _(Fixed same day — PR #794; see that bullet.)_
  - **Deferred — "Fix A" local-multiline fallback ordering on a none-resolved paste** `[housekeeping]` (Claude review PR #794, non-blocking): in the none-resolved case the result keeps `multiple_exercises_in_input`, so `err.multipleExercises` stays true and the pre-existing client-side local-multiline fallback (`public/app.js` ~3789) runs the crude local parser BEFORE the new specific-ask gate. The local parser's own `!multi.errors.length` clean-gate prevents mis-logging, so this is low-risk and pre-existing — but the ordering means a none-resolved paste the local parser happens to read cleanly logs via the LOCAL grammar instead of surfacing the server's specific ask. Revisit when that fallback path is next touched (consider running the specific-ask gate first, or deleting the local fallback once the server rescue has soaked).
- **Curls card-vs-advisory inconsistency `[polish]` (owner follow-up 07-02):** the ✓ confirmation card echoed `Curls` (parsed-name echo) while the advisory simultaneously said "I don't recognize 'Curls'" — two checks (set-parse vs catalog-enrichment) shown with contradictory confidence on one surface; if saved, the row writes `exercise: Curls` with `muscle_group: Unknown`. Catalog variants for BC01 are `Bicep Curl / Bicep Curls / Biceps Curl / Biceps Curls` — bare `Curls` is not among them ("Bicep curls" resolved cleanly in the same session). Two-part resolution: **(a) display — ✅ FIXED (review-card truthfulness PR, 2026-07-02):** when the unknown-lift advisory fires, the parsed name is remembered (`lastUnverifiedExercise`, `public/app.js`), threaded into the `atlas:set-logged` detail, and the confirmation card (`buildReadback`, `public/coach-conversation.js`) renders a "check name" chip next to the ✓ for that exercise (primary and additional lifts) — the card and the advisory now agree; the sets are still captured and the name stays editable before save. **Owner live-verified 2026-07-02 9:26 (IMG_5449): chip renders on the live ✓ Curls card alongside the advisory, shell v81 confirmed.** Same PR: the effort-only review intro no longer promises "the full session" (says plainly "no logged sets are in this save"), and an empty-string HR can no longer render as a bare "bpm" on the effort grid (empty ≡ null: Avg HR row drops, Peak HR shows "not in screenshot"). Shell v80→v81; introspection tests for all three. **(b) data — owner approved 2026-07-02**, but this cloud session has no Sheets credentials: owner runs `scripts/catalog-maintenance.js` (add variant `Curls` to canonical `Bicep Curl`, dry-run first, then `--confirm`) on a host with the Sheets env. Until the variant lands, bare `Curls` saves as `exercise: Curls` / `muscle_group: Unknown` — now clearly marked on the card.

## Bug remediation — 2026-06-29 live gym test (Bug_Reports tab, BUG-20260629)

Triaged from owner-logged bugs in the `Bug_Reports` Google Sheet tab (2026-06-26/29). Ordered by severity.

- ✅ **[tooling] Controlled `Exercise_Catalog` maintenance script** `[housekeeping]` — `scripts/catalog-maintenance.js` (owner-authorized 2026-06-29: "add or use a controlled catalog-maintenance command to append Exercise_Catalog rows"). Appends rows via the existing `sheets.js` client. **This is a new write-capable path to production Sheets** — deliberately gated: `Exercise_Catalog`-only (refuses other tabs; hard-blocks `Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`/`Bug_Reports`/…), dry-run by default, `--confirm` required to write, canonical+variant duplicate check, post-write read-back, never prints secrets. Pure planner unit-tested (13 cases) without creds. Production writes remain an explicit owner-initiated action (`--confirm` on a host that has the Sheets env). Does not touch runtime app code or the trust path.
  - **Deferred polish (review PR #693, non-blocking) `[housekeeping]`:** (1) dedup is name/variant-only — two rows with distinct names but the same `Lift_Code` could both write (catalog identity is by name, so low risk); (2) read-back verifies canonical-name presence only, not that `muscle_group`/`lift_code` landed in the right columns; (3) `getHeaderRow()` is redundant since `getExerciseCatalog()` already returns the header at index 0. Fold into a future authorized tooling touch — not worth a standalone PR.
- ✅ **[correctness] Bodyweight lifts no longer dead-end on "No recent working sets found for this lift code"** (PR #700) — `recommendNextSet` (`services/analytics.js`) filtered candidate working sets on a positive *weight*, which a bodyweight movement (hanging knee raises, push-ups) never has, so every set was dropped → false "no working sets" + no next card (BUG-20260629-153217 / 152824). Now gated on reps; a rep-based `isBodyweight` branch progresses by reps and never fabricates a load; bodyweight lifts emit a `next_target` so the card renders. Weighted lifts (incl. a stray trailing 0-weight set on a normally-weighted lift) stay on the load path via the `hasWeightedHistory` anchor. Read-only analytics path — no write/trust-loop/schema/proof-field change.
  - **Open follow-up (review PR #700, non-blocking) `[correctness]`:** a **first-ever in-workout bodyweight lift with NO history** still hits the original dead-end — when `rows.length === 0` the just-logged early-return anchor (`recommendNextSet` line ~613 → `recommendFromJustLoggedSet`) requires `isPositiveFinite(jl.weight)`, so a brand-new bodyweight set returns "No recent working sets found" + `next_target: null`. PR #700 fixed the read-back-with-history path (the reported live bug); this no-history just-logged case needs `recommendFromJustLoggedSet` to grow a rep-based branch. Distinct code path → deferred to a future authorized analytics PR.
- **[correctness] Multi-exercise freestyle is all-clean-or-drop — one ambiguous lift discards the clean siblings** `[deferred — trust-design]` — when a multi-exercise paste contains a genuinely ambiguous lift (e.g. `knee raises 20 15 15` — weight vs reps, deliberately clarified), the current `parseLogSets` returns `needs_clarification` for the **whole** block and logs none of the cleanly-parsed exercises (`services/workoutTextParser.js` ~line 560, "only return the combined result if EVERY chunk cleanly resolves"). This is deliberately conservative (never mis-log), but it reads to the lifter as "missed rows". A partial-log path (log the clean lifts into the preview, surface only the ambiguous one to re-enter) would resolve the remaining "missed rows" class — but it touches the preview→approve trust surface, so it is a **design decision for the owner**, not a silent change. **OWNER DECIDED 2026-07-02: partial-log.** Shipped for **newline-separated** pastes in the partial-log PR (see the ✅ 2026-07-02 re-test bullet in "Multi-workout composer" below): clean lines buffer, each ambiguous line surfaces its own specific ask. **Remaining scope:** inline same-line stacking still refuses-to-merge whole (G1 guardrail — partial parsing is unsafe without a reliable inline boundary; extend partial-log there only with the owner-gated KB-backed splitter lane), and a single-line single-exercise ambiguous input (e.g. `knee raises 20 15 15`) still asks whole, as designed.
