## Housekeeping

- **Curl taxonomy + multi-curl clarify (follow-ups to the bare-lead disambiguation PR)** `[correctness]` (owner-reserved): (1) the catalog has near-duplicate bicep-curl rows — "Bicep Curl" (BC01) and "Dumbbell Curl" (CRL01) — for effectively the same movement. This PR steers plain "curl"/"curls" to BC01 and the plan-aware client layer resolves bare terms to the planned curl, but "dumbbell curl" still resolves to its own CRL01 row. Folding Dumbbell Curl into Bicep Curl is a product/logging-model decision (recommendation engine names "Dumbbell Curl") — owner call; either merge the catalog rows or add `dumbbell curl → bicep curl` to `PREFERRED_ALIAS_TARGETS`. (2) When the plan has MULTIPLE exercises of one family (e.g. bicep + hammer curls), a bare "curls" is left unresolved (falls to the alias default). Owner floated an optional "ask which one" clarify step — build the clarify UX if the multi-curl case proves common. Both in `services/exerciseEnrichment.js` / `public/app.js`.
- **Completed-exercise read-back — lift-code / alias matching (follow-up to the plural-name fix)** `[correctness]`: `computePlanState` now matches completion by singular-normalized name + lift_code, which fixes the plural class ("Lateral Raises" ↔ "Lateral Raise"). It does NOT resolve abbreviations/aliases ("OHP" → "Overhead Press") or a substituted lift completing its plan slot ("Lateral Raise" logged against a "Face Pull" slot) — those surfaced in the live read-back bug (FR-20260706013655-4t692wuh, "It read back to me a couple workouts I already did… when we were talking about laterals"). The safe fix is to thread the authoritative **lift_code** end-to-end: `currentPlanForChat` and `plan_completed` currently send NAMES only (`public/app.js`), so the lift_code tier in `computePlanState` is never exercised for the chat's `plan_state`. Have the client send `{name, liftCode}` for `current_plan` and `plan_completed` (codes are available via the recommendation / `resolveCompletedIdentity`) so completion matches by code regardless of name form — no fuzzy guessing (which risks marking the WRONG exercise done). Touches the restricted `public/app.js` session surface → own focused PR; owner may want to pick the approach (lift-code vs. adding a conservative fuzzy tier).
- **Confirm-card "already saved this session" recap — two follow-ups (from PR #863 review)** `[housekeeping]`: (1) the `sessionSavedLog` ledger is in-memory only and not part of the session snapshot, so a page reload / PWA eviction after a mid-session save resets the recap to empty — the earlier-saved exercises would again look absent from the closeout card (data is safe on the Sheet; display-only convenience doesn't survive a reload). Persist it into the session snapshot if the reload case matters. (2) The recap sources exercise names from `sessionLog` at approve time rather than from the written payload (`collectLogRows`/edit table), so if the owner edits/removes rows in the preview before approving, the recap could list a slightly different name than what was written. Both display-only, low-impact; capture only. Suggested PR type: small `public/app.js` follow-up, own concern.
- **Flight Recorder `latestCoachMessage()` picks the last `.coach-msg` across all bubbles** `[housekeeping]` (from PR #864 review): a single coach bubble can hold multiple `.coach-msg` nodes (ack / effort-note / extra), so the captured `coach_message` may be a trailing effort-note rather than the main reply. Still a truthful "most-recent visible coach text" (acceptable for observability); if tighter fidelity is wanted, pick the FIRST `.coach-msg` of the LAST `.chat-bubble-atlas`. Display-only, non-blocking.
- **Branch protection on `main`** `[housekeeping]` (GitHub Settings → Branches): require PRs + required status checks + "require branches to be up to date before merging." What fails without it: stale branches can silently clobber shared files on merge. Why it matters: prevents accidental data loss in the shared handoff bus. Suggested PR type: GitHub settings change (no code); owner action only.
- **Coach-voice golden-output rubric** `[housekeeping]`: Extend `test/coach.test.js` into a rubric where a voice change must update the test — ends the per-PR phrasing loop. See [FIX_PLAN.md "Process changes"](./FIX_PLAN.md). What fails without it: voice changes pass tests without proving the new wording is correct. Suggested PR type: test-only PR; Sonnet 4.6.
- ◐ **Phase 3 dead-code cleanup** `[housekeeping]` (optional, low-priority): ✅ redundant condition `if (!previousPerformance.completed && previousPerformance.completed === false)` (`services/recommendationPolicy.js:143`) — AUDIT LO-10 — **done** (simplified to `completed === false`, behavior-identical for every value; regression tests added for the missed-target hold + the completed-set guard). ✅ **stale unused imports in `index.js` analytics destructure** (`buildWeeklyReport`/`buildProgressSummary`/`buildExerciseDetail`/`buildMuscleGroupReadiness` — each imported but never referenced; verified count=1) — AUDIT LO-4 — **done (this PR).** **Remaining (LO-3, deferred — NOT cleanly dead):** `loadTodaysPlan()` (`public/app.js`, targets the removed `#todays-plan` per `docs/TODAY_SCREEN_SPEC.md`) is uncalled at runtime, **but 7 source-introspection tests in `test/unit.test.js` pin its plan-card rendering** ("today's plan…", "start-any-lift: plan cards are tappable and call startLift", "Tap to start hint", etc.). Removing it cleanly therefore also means removing those 7 tests AND confirming the plan-card / `startLift` UI is truly gone for good (it touches the removed Today/Dashboard screen — Dashboard restoration is on the do-NOT-build list, so likely safe, but it's a deliberate call, not silent housekeeping). Do it as its own focused PR with that owner-aware framing; left in place here rather than hastily deleting tested behavior. Evidence: [`docs/AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-06-20.md`](./docs/AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-06-20.md). One concern per PR. See [FIX_PLAN.md Phase 3](./FIX_PLAN.md).
- **Dead enrichment alias `Lats → Lat Pulldown` in `exerciseEnrichment.js`** `[housekeeping]`: PR #335 demoted "lats" to a contextual alias — the parser now surfaces `needs_clarification` instead of emitting a "Lats" unknown-exercise row, so the enrichment-layer mapping is effectively unreachable for parser-originated rows. Harmless for now; remove in a future cleanup PR. Suggested PR type: one-line delete + regression test; Sonnet 4.6.
- **`spark.innerHTML = trendSparklineSvg(...)` in `public/app.js`** `[housekeeping]` (surfaced during PR-02 Claude review, 2026-07-06): one remaining `innerHTML` assignment injects generated SVG markup from `trendSparklineSvg()`, which is driven by numeric series data — not user-controlled text — so it's not the same `err.message` injection vector PR-02 targeted and was correctly left out of scope. If a future sweep hardens all `innerHTML` sinks, replace with `svgEl.outerHTML` assigned via a proper SVG DOM builder, or trust the fully-controlled numeric series and document the deliberate exception. Low risk; SVG-specific; own concern.
- **Merge the two backend-expansion completion records** `[housekeeping]` — `EXPANSION_SUMMARY.md` and `COMPLETION_CHECKLIST.md` are near-duplicate point-in-time records of the same 12-endpoint backend expansion. Both are now banner'd HISTORICAL (planning-consolidation PR-6). **Merge candidate:** fold into a single historical record. **Deferred — the merge/deletion is its own PR** (do not delete docs autonomously; a doc-removal is owner-visible cleanup). Filed from [`docs/PLANNING_DOCS_AUDIT_2026-07-01.md`](./docs/PLANNING_DOCS_AUDIT_2026-07-01.md) (recommendation G).
- **Deeper BACKLOG re-section (physical)** `[housekeeping]` — planning-consolidation PR-7 added an additive "▶ Active / Now" navigation block at the top, but deferred the *physical* restructure the audit's Q9 describes: relocate the strategic/owner-gated sections (Multi-style PLANNING, One-Brain owner-gated prerequisites, Someday, Strategic direction) beneath one hard divider, collapse the multiple "TOP PRIORITY" lane headers into a single ordered block, and convert remaining multi-paragraph items to one-line-+-link per this file's own charter. **Deferred deliberately:** a large reorder of the source-of-truth file is high-risk for silent content loss and warrants a focused, owner-visible pass rather than an automated batch. Filed from [`docs/PLANNING_DOCS_AUDIT_2026-07-01.md`](./docs/PLANNING_DOCS_AUDIT_2026-07-01.md) (recommendation F / PR-7). **Update (PR-21, 2026-07-08):** the governance-diet pass mechanically archived every fully-closed, self-contained (✅/`SHIPPED`-marked, no nested follow-up) item into `BACKLOG_ARCHIVE.md` (~57 KB moved) — safe because it never had to separate a still-open follow-up from its shipped parent. `BACKLOG.md` is still ~460 KB, well past the plan's original ≤40 KB target; closing that gap needs exactly this item's editorial pass (splitting a shipped item's historical prose from its live follow-up note) and a per-file byte-cap CI check once the size is real. Still deferred for the same reason: too risky to automate in one pass.

## Automation framework follow-ups

> **Merge-authority update (owner ruling, Dale 2026-07-15).** Claude now holds **standing merge authority** and merges its own PRs once CI hard gates pass and Codex advisory findings are addressed — no owner merge step. Cold review is **retired** as a required gate (Codex advisory + Claude's optional internal review, neither a required human sign-off). `CLAUDE.md` was updated and **supersedes** conflicting language elsewhere. **Open follow-ups:**
> - ✅ **DONE (F04A, 2026-07-16):** the owner removed `cold-review/exact-head` from `main`'s required status checks, and F04A deleted the compatibility mechanism entirely — `.github/workflows/cold-review-gate.yml` + `scripts/cold-review-gate.js` + `test/cold-review-gate.test.js` + `docs/COLD_REVIEW_GATE.md` — plus every stale reference. No code can publish `cold-review/exact-head`; no doc mandates a compatibility marker. `risk-label/primary` remains the only added status (not resurrected as a review lane).
> - **Doc reconciliation `[infrastructure]`:** propagate the new merge-authority/cold-review model into `docs/AUTOMATION_PROTOCOL.md`, `docs/AGENT_WORKFLOW.md`, `docs/OWNER_CHECKIN_RULES.md`, `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md` (+ `merge-card-check.yml` sentinels), `docs/RISK_LABELS.md`, and `docs/DOCS_INDEX.md` so they no longer say "cold review required / stop for Dale to merge / owner is the only merge authority." `CLAUDE.md`'s supersession clause governs in the meantime.

The automation-first contract shipped as documentation + templates + label manifest + working GitHub Actions enforcement (`docs/AUTOMATION_PROTOCOL.md`, `docs/OWNER_CHECKIN_RULES.md`, `docs/RISK_LABELS.md`, `docs/AUTOMATION_AUDIT.md`, `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md`, `.github/labels.yml`, and the workflows below). Source: `docs/AUTOMATION_AUDIT.md`.

Shipped in the framework PR (#422):

Shipped after #422:

- ~~**Exact-head cold-review gate** `[infrastructure]`~~ — ✅ **RETIRED & REMOVED (F04A, 2026-07-16).** The `cold-review/exact-head` status workflow/script/logic-test/doc that enforced the exact-head cold-review marker rule are deleted; the owner removed the required-check binding. Codex review is advisory and an optional clean-context review is confidence-only — neither is a required status, marker, or identity gate. Its two open hardening follow-ups (reviewer-identity allowlist ≠ builder; thread-resolution awareness) are moot — there is no gate to harden.

Remaining (owner-named PR-checks follow-ups — build in this order, each a tiny one-concern PR):

- ✅ **Primary-risk-label gate** `[infrastructure]` — SHIPPED (the 2nd owner-named "biggest hole"): `.github/workflows/risk-label-gate.yml` + `scripts/risk-label-gate.js` fail unless **exactly one** of `auto-safe` / `owner-live-test` / `owner-decision` / `blocked` is applied (per `AUTOMATION_PROTOCOL.md` §2), re-running on every label change. Same secure pattern as the cold-review gate (`pull_request_target`, logic from the default branch only, fail-closed) and, like it, publishes a `risk-label/primary` commit status on the PR head SHA (a `pull_request_target` job conclusion would not attach to the head that branch protection evaluates). Auto-labeling (`labeler.yml`) stays non-blocking. Owner step to bind it: add the `risk-label/primary` status check to `main` required status checks. **Deferred sub-note:** the merge-card `Risk level: low/medium/high` prose field was intentionally left as-is — it reads as a severity feel, a different axis from the who-acts primary label the gate enforces; collapsing the two is a governance-wording call, file separately if the owner wants it.
- **Dependency review on lockfile changes** `[infrastructure]` — add `actions/dependency-review-action`, gated to PRs that change `package-lock.json` / `package.json`, to block a bump that introduces a vulnerable or malicious dependency. **Distinct** from the "Add Dependabot" item below (that is *update tooling*; this is a *PR gate*). Required only when dependencies change.
- **CodeQL security scan on runtime-code changes** `[infrastructure]` — add a CodeQL (JavaScript) workflow as a PR check for common server-side issues (injection, unsafe data handling, auth mistakes). Scope to runtime-code changes.
- **Render post-deploy `/version` verification + read-only smoke on `main`** `[infrastructure]` — the render-smoke job (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `scripts/smoke-test-render.js`) runs on `main`/dispatch with `expected_version` blank by default; after a merge it should confirm the deployed `/version` matches the merged commit SHA **before** running the production read-only suite, so "GitHub green while Render still serves the old/broken build" is caught. Post-merge/`main` only; stays skipped on docs-only PRs. Coverage stays report-only (owner decision) — no blunt global coverage gate.

Remaining:

- **Verify the Decision Desk's author-association gate on the first live request** `[infrastructure]` — the desk triggers only when the Decision Request comment's `author_association` ∈ `OWNER/MEMBER/COLLABORATOR`. If the builder posts the request under a GitHub App/bot identity, the association can be `NONE`/`CONTRIBUTOR`, so the desk never fires (safe — fail-closed, never an implicit "yes" — but silently inert). On the first live request, confirm the posting identity's association lands in the allowed set; if not, add a **precise login allowlist** for the builder bot to the workflow `if` (do NOT loosen the gate to allow `NONE` broadly). Surfaced by automated review of #426.

A `.github/dependabot.yml` config (weekly cadence, minor/patch updates grouped into one PR, security alerts always separate) routes bumps through the standard Atlas pipeline (lint + unit tests + E2E + merge card). Low noise when configured with grouping. No app/schema/write-path behavior change. **Do after current P0/roadmap work settles.** (Surfaced by 2026-06-26 CI workflow audit.)
- **Optional OpenAI-backed Codex responder** `[infrastructure]` `owner-decision` — a drop-in responder mode for the decision desk using `CODEX_OPENAI_API_KEY` + `CODEX_MODEL` instead of the subscription token. **DECISION (owner 2026-06-25): DEFERRED INDEFINITELY — no concrete need exists. The subscription-token responder is the default and sufficient.** (Paid OpenAI API cost not justified without a specific need.)

Convention-enforced merge discipline is sufficient at current team scale.** Supersedes/absorbs the existing "Branch protection on `main`" housekeeping item above.

Amend PR1/PR2 to explicitly recognize the infrastructure category (distinct from docs and from app-code PRs) so the standing invariants and the new contract do not disagree. **Owner-gated** because it edits `docs/INVARIANTS.md` (Constitution layer — OWNER_CHECKIN_RULES criterion 5); not self-amended in #422. Surfaced by automated review of #422.

## Multi-style training support — PLANNING (owner-gated, not active)

Atlas is currently shaped around one lifter's style (gym progressive overload in `weight × reps @ RIR`). The Dream is a training-intelligence engine for **many** styles. Planning spec filed: [`docs/TRAINING_PROFILE_TAXONOMY.md`](./docs/TRAINING_PROFILE_TAXONOMY.md) — defines the first five training profiles (strength · hypertrophy · general fitness · cardio/endurance · bodyweight/calisthenics/circuits), a deterministic **profile-score** model (style as a multi-axis vector, not one fixed label), an onboarding classifier, an exercise **modality schema** (`load_type`/`fatigue_class`/`effort_metric`/`progression_metric`), expanded logging grammar (duration/distance/pace/zone/rounds/`+load`), a **profile-aware stimulus governor** (RIR rules differ by profile; RIR is irrelevant for cardio), live cross-modality fatigue routing, coach-voice examples, and a per-profile golden test matrix. Research-grounded (WHO 2020 / HHS activity guidelines; ACSM progression; Zourdos 2016 RIR-RPE; Refalo 2023 proximity-to-failure; HR-zone / Karvonen models).

**Status:** docs/plan only — no production, write-path, parser, or schema change. **Owner-gated to promote.** Phased engine-first build plan (PR-T2…PR-T8) lives in the spec's Part D. Scope fence: this is multi-*style* for the single owner, **not** multi-*user* (multi-user stays out per the Vision guardrail). Promote a slice into the active campaign ([`docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`](./docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md)) only on explicit owner direction.

- **PR-T2** modality schema (pure data) `[correctness]` · **PR-T3** profile-score engine (read-only) `[correctness]` · **PR-T4** onboarding classifier `[correctness]` · **PR-T5** profile-aware stimulus governor `[trust-critical]` · **PR-T6** expanded logging grammar — **owner-gated** (slash-notation contract / `workoutTextParser.js`) + the **[owner-gated migration]** decision for where non-RIR metrics persist `[trust-critical]` · **PR-T7** live fatigue routing `[correctness]` · **PR-T8** coach voice `[polish]`.

**Session Planning Engine — PLANNING (owner-gated, not active).** Planning spec filed: [`docs/SESSION_PLANNING_ENGINE.md`](./docs/SESSION_PLANNING_ENGINE.md) — documents how Atlas picks the **best session today** (not just "what's next"): daily **objective-scoring model** (`profile_match`/`goal_match`/`equipment_fit`/`time_fit`/`recovery_fit`/`adherence_fit`/`movement_balance_need` minus `pain_conflict`/`fatigue_conflict`; weights are initial defaults, fixture-protected before shipping), ten supported **session objectives** (strength_progression · hypertrophy_volume · general_fitness_balanced · cardio_base · cardio_intervals · bodyweight_strength · circuit_conditioning · skill_practice · recovery_reload · deload), deterministic **exercise-order rules** + exceptions, planning + fatigue-adjusted examples, a golden-fixture test matrix, and the boundaries vs the Taxonomy / Modality Schema / Stimulus Governor / Live Fatigue Router / Coach Voice / Recovery-Deload systems. Builds on the Training Profile Taxonomy (the planner is the profile-aware front end over the existing `scoreIntents` / `structureSession` machinery). Also documents a **recovery/deload model** (grounded in the *Deloading in Resistance Training* report): convergence-based deload triggers (no deload after one bad session), default deload prescription, profile-aware deload styles, and the boundary between normal fatigue routing · `recovery_reload` · `deload` · taper · maintenance · complete rest — **planning-layer selection only; does not change `docs/DELOAD_SPEC.md` / `computePrescription`**. **Docs/plan only** — no production, parser, write-path, or schema change; **owner-gated to promote** (do not add it to the active campaign — [`docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`](./docs/ATLAS_V1_EXECUTION_PLAN.md) — until promoted). Suggested first slices once promoted: objective-scoring engine (pure, read-only) → fixture-pin the weights → objective→order builder, deterministic-first.

### Training Intelligence Implementation Series (owner-approved slices)

The build sequence that implements the Training Profile Taxonomy + Session Planning Engine planning specs. **PR 477 is promoted to `docs/ACTIVE_ROADMAP.md` and is the only active slice.** All later slices stay **owner-gated / not active** — promote one at a time on explicit owner direction. (The `PR 47x` labels are the owner's planned *logical* slice numbers; actual GitHub PR numbers are a separate namespace and may differ — see the roadmap's "Roadmap Step numbers vs GitHub PR numbers" note.)

- **PR 477 — RIR-aware coach accountability + live pressing fatigue routing** `[trust-critical]` — **ACTIVE** (promoted). Behavior-changing, trust-sensitive; **current weighted/RIR workflow only**. Detects warmup/feeder vs work vs redline (RIR 0) sets, rep-drop-after-redline, high-RIR underdosed work sets; marks pressing (chest/triceps/front-delts · horizontal_push/vertical_push) intra-session yellow; routes the next planned press (pull-first reroute / cap / optional) after a bench redline. Reuses `effortVerdict`/`recommendNextSet`/`patternFor`/`musclesFor`/`classifyLiftRole` + the existing coach reaction channel. No parser/schema/write-path change.
  - ✅ **PR-B (wiring + deterministic copy)** — *shipped.* Reason codes registered centrally (`services/trainingKnowledge.js`); new pure `services/setEffortCopy.js` (deterministic `effortNote`/`rerouteNote`); engine run in the `/api/coach/message` set path (gated on weighted/RIR sets + a non-empty planned queue), returning additive `effort_note` + suggestion-only `reroute`; `public/app.js` threads the remaining `plannedQueue`; `public/coach-conversation.js` renders one short effort line + folds the reroute into the existing handoff. No parser/schema/write-path/trust-loop change; LLM prompt untouched. Tests: `test/setEffortCopy.test.js`, api-smoke live-path + `unit.test.js` source-introspection.

      - `[correctness]` Cardio / bodyweight-only sets (no finite weight/RIR) produce **no** effort line by design — the set-effort engine is RIR/weight-only. Multi-modality effort reads land in **PR 486+**.

Align the placeholder with the reroute if live use wants it.

Coach-surface; building as tiny read-only slices (no LLM-number invention; preview→approve→write untouched).

Tests: renderer goldens, real-route api-smoke (lecture suppressed, deterministic line owns it, unconfigured fallback, downgrade-not-suppressed), template goldens. Full suite green (2354). No parser/schema/write-path/proof-field/trust-loop change; LLM prompt untouched.
    - **Deferred (review nit, not built):** `/api/coach/chat` does not yet run the substitution contradiction guard — Slice 2 covers the set/in-workout `/api/coach/message` surface (where the swap is worded today). If chat starts narrating swaps, route its prose through `findSubstitutionContradictions` too.
  - ✅ **Slice 3 — "I'm tired" recovery routing** `[correctness]` — *this PR (owner go 2026-06-23).* When the lifter SAYS they're tired/cooked/drained on the **chat** surface, the deterministic engine now OWNS the reply and routes on the actual recovery state — the LLM is bypassed so it can never default to motivation hype. New pure `services/recoveryRouting.js`: `isTirednessExpression` (tight first-person fatigue detector; excludes "tired of …" impatience, negation "I'm not tired", analytical questions "why am I tired?", and unrelated uses) + `buildTirednessRecoveryAnswer` (grounds in `computeFatigueStatus` weekly-load status, `assessLayoff` days-since, and fatigued readiness patterns; never hypes; invents no lift-specific numbers). Wired into `POST /api/coach/chat` (read-only): short-circuits before `generateChatReply` on the configured path (grounded) and replaces the dead-end on the unconfigured path (safe recovery line). Verified current-state: the SME returns `log_only`/no-answer for tiredness, so these messages genuinely reach the chat surface as the item described. No prompt change (owner "no broad AI prompt changes"), no parser/schema/write-path/proof-field/trust-loop change; backend-only (no SW bump). Tests: `test/recoveryRouting.test.js` (detector positives/negatives, every signal branch, never-hype guard, no-fabricated-numbers) + real-route api-smoke (LLM hype bypassed, unconfigured no dead-end, non-tired still reaches LLM, read-only). Full suite green (2366).
    - **Deferred (enhancement):** the reply is deterministic (warm but fixed per signal case). If a more conversational tone is wanted later, route it through `coachPolish` with numbers/meaning locked, or add a varied-phrasing bank — keep the recovery routing + no-hype guarantee.
    - **Deferred (review nits, non-blocking):** (1) a fatigue statement with a trailing *question* clause ("I'm tired, should I still do legs?") short-circuits to the recovery line via the leading "I'm" rather than answering the lift question — the advice is still reasonable, but a refinement could defer to the SME/coach when a question clause is present. (2) `buildTirednessRecoveryAnswer` only names `fatigued` patterns; if `recovering` (the next-tier readiness status) should also count toward "tired" routing, include it and adjust the wording (it currently says "flagged fatigued").
  - **Deferred — `hard` gets its own praise line** `[polish]` (review nit): `hard` is now its own severity (slice 1b) but still reuses the `on_target` praise line as its deterministic floor, and unlike `on_target` it does NOT suppress the LLM (so the LLM normally words it). Give `hard` a distinct short line (e.g. "Right at your target — strong, controlled work.") only if `hard` sets should read differently from dialled-in `on_target`.
- ✅ **PR 486 — Multi-modality logging — COMPLETE** `[trust-critical, owner-gated parser]` (closed out 2026-06-24, owner live-validated). Cardio / bodyweight / holds / AMRAP / EMOM / circuits all ship end-to-end: recognition (slices 1, 2, 2b) → dry-run preview wiring (slice 3) → `Modality_Log` optional-tab schema + trust-loop write route + hardening (slices 4a–4c) → frontend preview→approve→write wiring (slice 5) → slice-3 follow-up edge resolved (below). The **slash-notation contract** (`225 5/2`) is unchanged; non-slash modalities are recognized **additively** in `services/multiModalityParser.js`, never by altering `workoutTextParser.js`. **Owner live-validated 2026-06-24** (tab created, cardio preview correct, write succeeded, row correct, strength logging unaffected). **Remaining are explicitly-deferred speculative edges only** (no owner input needed, build only if such input appears): unknown-bodyweight-movement `+load` (line below) and `weight+addedload` notation. No further 486 work is scheduled.
  - ✅ **Slice 1 — recognition module (pure, unwired)** *(shipped)*: `services/multiModalityParser.js::recognizeModalityInput(text)` recognizes the non-slash formats — **timed holds** (`Plank 60 sec RPE 7 x3` → duration_sec/sets/rpe) and **steady cardio** (`Elliptical 30 min RPE 6 avg HR 142`; `Run 5 km 32:10 ...` → duration_min/distance_km/elapsed/level/rpe/avg_hr; miles→km) into structured fields. **Returns null for any weighted/slash input** so the resistance parser contract is never hijacked (golden negative controls: Bench/Squat/Lat Pulldown/Pushups slash inputs → null). Pure: no I/O / write-path / schema / parser-grammar change; not wired. Tests: `test/multiModalityParser.test.js`. Full suite green.
  - ✅ **Slice 2 — intervals + circuits recognition (pure, unwired)** *(shipped)*: extends `recognizeModalityInput` with **cardio intervals** (`8 x 400m / 90 sec`, `Run intervals 4 x 1 mile / 3 min RPE 8`, time-based `6 x 2 min / 60 sec` → `{rounds, work_distance_m|work_sec, rest_sec, rpe, avg_hr}`; runs **before** steady cardio so a rounds pattern isn't read as steady distance) and **circuits** (`AMRAP 12 min: pushups 10, … - 6 rounds RPE 8`, `EMOM 10 min: …` → `{kind, cap_min, rounds, movements[], rpe, avg_hr}`; circuit keyword wins over a hold-name listed inside it). Added `cardio_interval`/`circuit` to `MODALITIES`. Folded two slice-1 review nits: timed-hold `sets` now matches a **mid-string** `x3`/`3x` multiplier (not only trailing), and the steady-cardio doc comment no longer lists an unsupported `pace` field. **Slash-notation contract guard intact** (returns null for any `\d/\d` set input). Pure: no I/O / write-path / schema / parser-grammar change; not wired. Tests: `test/multiModalityParser.test.js` (intervals + circuits goldens, mid-string xN, vocab). Full suite green (2489).
  - ✅ **Slice 3 — wire recognition into the dry-run preview (no write)** *(shipped, owner-authorized 2026-06-23)*: `POST /api/parse-workout-text` (the dry-run-only text preview) now attaches recognized non-slash **modality** metadata (timed holds / steady cardio / intervals / circuits) to the response via `recognizeModalityInput`, **additively and read-only** — attached in the route handler (alongside `kb_identity`), so `services/workoutTextParser.js` (the slash grammar) is untouched. **Slash contract intact:** modality is attached only when the resistance parse did **not** claim `log_sets`, and the recognizer already returns null for slash input, so `Bench 225 5/2` gets a `log_sets` parse and **no** modality. **No write / no schema / dry-run proof fields (`test_mode`/`sheet_written:false`/`no_write_confirmed:true`) untouched** (best-effort try/catch — recognition can never break the dry-run). Folded the review-nit test lock: a unitless `N x N` (`Bench 3 x 5`) → null (not interval/circuit). Tests: `test/multiModalityParser.test.js` (unitless negative control) + `test/api-smoke.test.js` (real route: circuit + cardio attach with no write; slash set → no modality, no write). Full suite green (2494).
    - ✅ **Resolved (slice-3 follow-up, review edge) (2026-06-24, 486 close-out):** `recognizeCircuit` (`services/multiModalityParser.js`) now returns null for a **bare** `circuit` keyword (not AMRAP/EMOM/WOD/metcon) that has **no circuit structure** (no colon movement list, no explicit rounds) AND reads as steady cardio (a `CARDIO_NAME` machine/locomotion match) — so `Bike circuit 20 min` falls through to `cardio_steady` instead of being filed as a `circuit` now that persistence routes by modality. Strong conditioning formats (AMRAP/EMOM/WOD/metcon), structured circuits (`Bike circuit 5 rounds`, `…: movements`), and a non-cardio bare circuit (`Circuit 15 min`) are all unaffected. Golden tests added in `test/multiModalityParser.test.js`. Pure recognizer change; no schema/write-path/slash-parser change.
  - ✅ **Slice 4a — `Modality_Log` typed tab schema (pure config, no write path)** *(shipped, autonomous within authorized 486 scope)*: defines the persistence contract for recognized non-slash modalities as a **new OPTIONAL typed sibling tab** (the documented default target, option b), mirroring `Constraints`/`Deload_State`. `config/columns.js` adds `modalityLogColumns` (12: `date, session_id, modality, exercise, duration_sec, distance_m, rounds, rest_sec, level, rpe, avg_hr, notes`) + `modalityLogRowFieldAliases`; the `modality` column discriminates how the shared metric columns are read (hold/steady/interval/circuit). `config/sheetContract.js` registers `Modality_Log` in `optionalSheetTabs` — **absent → sheet still complete** (the future write route returns 503 until it exists). **Leaves 12-col `Log_Cleaned` / 9-col `Effort` untouched; no write path; no data written** — slice 4a defines the contract only. Tests: `test/unit.test.js` (optional-not-required, pinned 12-col order + aliases, header validation incl. camelCase + wrong-position). Full suite green (2497). **Column design is owner-reviewable before slice 4b wires any write.**
  - ✅ **Slice 2b — added-load bodyweight slash logging** *(shipped, owner-authorized 2026-06-23)*: `Dips +25 8/2`, `Weighted dips +50 10/2 x3`, `Pull-ups +25 6/2`, `Chin-ups +10 8/2` now log within the existing slash-notation contract. The minimal, contained change (one line in `parseSetGroups`, `services/workoutTextParser.js`): a `+` immediately preceding a load is dropped so the EXTERNAL load reads as **Weight** (Atlas has no separate added-load column → schema unchanged), the movement NAME is unchanged (all four are known aliases), and reps/RIR/`xN` parse exactly like a normal slash set. Bodyweight is NOT auto-added. **Slash contract preserved:** no "+" input is byte-identical to before (`Bench 225 5/2 x3` unchanged); modality inputs (`Plank 60 sec x3`, `Elliptical 30 min`) stay NON-slash (recognized additively by `multiModalityParser.js`, untouched). No parser rewrite, no normal-weighted-lift change, no modality/schema/write-path/coach change; no frontend change. Tests: `parser-golden.test.js` (all four added-load examples + `Bench 225 5/2 x3` unchanged + modality stays non-slash). Full suite green (2535).
    - **Deferred (future, if needed):** the four targets are known aliases so they route through `parseSetGroups`; an *unknown* bodyweight movement with `+load` would also need the `+` handled in `looksLikeSetToken`/`extractUnknownExerciseLead` (the unknown-exercise lead). Out of scope for this slice — add only if such an input appears.
    - **Scope of the `+` strip (review #525, then hardened):** the strip runs on every input through `parseSetGroups` (known-alias + unknown-exercise paths), but is anchored to a token-LEADING `+` (start-of-text / after-space), so a `+` wedged between digits (`225+25`) is left alone and can never silently concatenate into a bogus weight. If a real `weight+addedload` (`225+25 5/2`) or `BW+25` notation is ever wanted, it needs its own explicit handling — the current strip deliberately does NOT interpret it.
    - **Deferred (audit PR 2 micro-nit, review #528 — benign):** the malformed-set guard would also intercept a *question* that simultaneously (a) escapes the backend AC8 question-floor (returns `needs_clarification`, not `intent:'question'`), (b) resolves a recognized resistance exercise, AND (c) contains `\d+/\d+` (e.g. "should I drop bench to the 3/4 range?"). Narrow (all three at once) and the failure mode is benign (a format hint vs a coach reply); the reviewer accepted it. If it ever shows up, add `!looksLikeModalityQuestion(pendingChatText)` to the guard condition (the detector already exists) so a question-shaped input is always excluded.
  - ✅ **Follow-up Fix A — multi-line, one-exercise-per-line strength logging** *(shipped, owner-authorized)*: a SECOND-round live test surfaced that a single multi-line message (`Bench 225 5/2 x3` / `Weighted dips +50 10/2 x3` / `Seated row 190 10/2 x3`) logged only ONE exercise. Root cause (confirmed): the backend INTENTIONALLY blocks a multi-exercise blob (`hasMultipleExerciseMentions` → `multiple_exercises_in_input`); since `extractSetParagraphs` splits only on BLANK lines, single-newline lines collapse to one multi-exercise line → blocked → the client (`noFallback`) dropped it to the coach, which summarized all three convincingly but buffered nothing (same masking class as Bugs #1/#2; new trigger). Fix (client-only, no backend-parser change): `rowsFromBackendParsedWorkout` flags `err.multipleExercises`; `rowsFromWorkoutInput` routes a `multiple_exercises_in_input` rejection to the existing line-based local parser **only when** the input spans newlines AND the local parse is CLEAN (no per-line errors) — so all lines log. **Same-line mixing (no newline) stays blocked** (backend guard untouched); an uncertain line is never silently logged (falls through). No write-path/schema/modality/coach-prompt change. SW shell v36→v37. Tests: behavioral (the pure local parser run on the exact failing input → 9 rows / 3 exercises / 0 errors; single line → 1 exercise) + source-introspection (branch gated on newline + clean parse, runs before the coach drop) + the existing backend same-line-block golden is preserved. Updated `clarification_blocks_local_parser_invocation` to permit ONLY this guarded exception. Full suite green (2542).
  - ✅ **Follow-up Fix B — bodyweight dips alias** *(shipped, owner-authorized)*: round-2 live test found `Dips 10/2 x3` returned "Could not find sets for Dips (Weighted)". Root cause: the `dips`/`dip` alias resolves to the canonical `Dips (Weighted)` (added load is the common case), forcing a weighted interpretation; with no weight token, `parseSetGroups` finds no sets → clarification. Fix (surgical, mirrors the existing `Hanging Knee Raises` bodyweight branch): in `parseLogSets`, when the resolved exercise is `Dips (Weighted)` AND the weighted slash parser finds **no** sets (no external load), re-label to a bodyweight `Dips` and read the reps via `parseBodyweightReps` (weight null). **Weighted dips unchanged** — `Dips +25 8/2` / `Weighted dips +50 10/2 x3` still resolve `Dips (Weighted)` with their load (parseSetGroups finds the load → the bodyweight branch is skipped). All Dips variants share lift code `DIP01` in `exerciseEnrichment.js`, so bodyweight vs added-load differ only by the weight value, not the exercise. No write-path/schema/modality/coach change; backend-only (no SW bump). Tests: `parser-golden.test.js` (bare `Dips`/`Dip` → bodyweight Dips weight-null; weighted dips preserved; mixed bodyweight + weighted + bench session). Full suite green (2546).

- **[Future enhancement, owner-gated] — Exercise-classification-aware fatigue model** `[training-intelligence, correctness]`

  **Status:** Deferred future enhancement. Not a regression or P0. Promote only on explicit owner direction.

  **Lane:** Training Intelligence (follows the PR 477–486 series). No active queue slot yet.

  **Live evidence (2026-06-26):** Heavy Back Squat sessions correctly leave Legs in a high-fatigue state. Accessory work such as Hanging Knee Raises currently contributes a similar "Worked" status for Core, even though the real systemic recovery cost is much lower. The recommendation and coaching surfaces treat both equally because the current readiness model sees only *when* a pattern was trained and *how hard* (via `minRir`), not *what kind of exercise* created that training stress.

  **Root cause (current design):**
  - `services/analytics.js::buildMuscleGroupReadiness` groups all logged sets by movement pattern and runs `recoveryFraction(daysSince, minRir, effortTauMultiplier(intensity))`. This correctly decays harder sessions slower (lower `minRir` → longer tau), but it does not distinguish a heavy compound at RIR 2 from an isolation accessory at the same RIR. A Knee Raise session with `minRir 2` and a Back Squat session with `minRir 2` produce the same recovery tau even though their systemic cost differs by an order of magnitude.
  - `services/muscleVolume.js::weeklyMuscleVolume` credits every set with a flat weight (1.0 primary / 0.5 secondary) regardless of exercise classification, load, or systemic cost.
  - `services/underCoverage.js::computeUnderCoverage` derives `adequate`/`under`/`optimal` status from total effective sets vs MEV targets — no exercise classification weighting. An accessory-only Core week can read as `adequate` even when no meaningful progressive overload was applied.

  **Existing infrastructure already in place (no new vocabulary needed):**
  - `services/exerciseModality.js::modalityFor` already emits a `fatigue_class` field (`high_cns`, `moderate_systemic`, `local`, `metabolic`, `aerobic`, `anaerobic`) for every exercise — but this field is currently unused in the readiness / volume / coverage / recommendation path.
  - `services/liftRole.js` classifies exercises as compound vs accessory; its module comment explicitly flags this as "a stopgap until the Exercise_Catalog carries explicit lift_role / fatigue_class metadata."
  - `services/stimulusGovernor.js::gradeStimulus` already applies different fatigue signals for heavy compounds (`high` on RIR 0) vs isolation (`elevated` or `ok`), and `services/stimulusGovernorRules.js` encodes those distinctions — the vocabulary and rules exist; they are just not wired into the readiness/recommendation path.
  - `recoveryFraction` in `services/analytics.js` already parameterizes the exponential decay tau by `minRir` and `effortTauMultiplier` — extending it with a third axis (`fatigue_class` multiplier) would be a small, contained change.

  **Desired future behavior:**
  - Heavy compounds (`high_cns`: Back Squat, Deadlift, Bench Press, Overhead Press) should produce a longer effective recovery tau — the readiness status reads `fatigued` or `recovering` for proportionally longer.
  - Accessories and isolation work (`local`: Knee Raises, Curls, Face Pulls, Shrugs) should produce a shorter tau — the same readiness status clears to `ready` / `fresh` in 1–2 days regardless of RIR.
  - `moderate_systemic` exercises (rows, dips, pull-ups, Romanian Deadlifts) fall between the two.
  - Workout recommendations (`scoreIntents`, `suggestedWorkout`) and coaching surfaces consume this richer fatigue model, so suggestions are based on meaningful recovery state rather than raw session recency.
  - No write-path, no schema, no parser change. Read-only engine enhancement only.

  **Proposed implementation direction (not designed or approved — speculative):**
  - Add a `fatigue_class` multiplier to `recoveryFraction`'s tau in `buildMuscleGroupReadiness`: derive `fatigue_class` via `modalityFor(canonical_exercise).fatigue_class` for the dominant (highest-volume) exercise in the session pattern bucket. Suggested initial ratios (fixture-protected before any wiring): `high_cns` → 1.4× tau, `moderate_systemic` → 1.0× (unchanged baseline), `local` → 0.7× tau, `metabolic`/`aerobic` → 0.5× tau.
  - Optionally introduce a parallel `weightedMuscleVolume` that applies the same `fatigue_class` multiplier to per-set effective-set credit in `muscleVolume.js`, consumed by `computeUnderCoverage` so the `adequate`/`under` status reflects exercise quality, not just set count.
  - The two changes are separable: the readiness tau extension is smaller-scoped and higher-signal; the volume credit extension is broader. Build the tau extension first.

  **Why deferred:** Not a regression — Atlas gives broadly correct recommendations today, and the existing `minRir`-based tau already captures the hardest effort in a session. This is a depth enhancement: the gap is between a hard compound session and a hard isolation session at the same RIR, which the current model cannot distinguish. Requires owner direction, a design pass on multiplier values, and golden-fixture protection before any wiring. Build after the active P0 queue and Trust-Critical Coach Interaction Lane are stable.

  **Owner gate:** Promote to the active roadmap and approve the `fatigue_class` tau multiplier table before implementation. This is a new training-intelligence scope addition, not a repair of a failing behavior.
