## Soul Plan PR-B1 — mode gaps the coaching-mode enum makes visible (2026-07-10)

`services/coachMode.js` (PR-B1, pure/unwired) names every coaching moment; mapping the EXISTING triggers exposed these gaps and recorded readings:

- ◐ **`[correctness]` chat drift engine trigger — PR-B5a Part 1 SHIPPED, Part 2 (wiring) remaining.** Part 1: pure `services/driftSignal.js` `detectDrift(logRows, plannedVsCompleted, memoryPatterns, { asOf, deloadState })` → `{ drifting, kind: skipped_pattern_streak|plan_deviation|sandbag_persistence, evidence }`, with deload/layoff/thin-history guards and conservative tuned thresholds (pattern skipped ≥3 consecutive planned sessions; ≥50% plan deviation over 3 sessions; consistent_underperformance with ≥3 sessions below). Fixtures in `test/driftSignal.test.js`; staged in the wiring allowlist (expires 2026-10-31). **Part 2 (next slice) — wiring:** assemble the `plannedVsCompleted` per-session history (from the Missed-Lift / Step-365 planned-vs-completed memory) + the `memoryPatterns` array + the current `Deload_State`, call `detectDrift` on the chat path, feed its output into `selectCoachMode`'s `challenge` trigger (extend `coachMode.js` mapping + precedence tests — today only `consistent_underperformance` reaches challenge; drift's three kinds should too), forward the evidence through the chat whitelist, and add the persona-core **challenge block** (Motivational-Interviewing shape: state the pattern with its numbers, then ASK — never lecture; the don't-cave rule when the user pushes back). Behavior tests: a drifting fixture → challenge mode with evidence; a user-disagrees follow-up keeps the position; no drift → no challenge.
  - **🛑 PREMISE FINDING — B5 Part-2 wiring is a knot of prerequisites, NOT a tiny diff (2026-07-10, evidence-based; do not rush-build).** Verified against `main` after B5a/B5b Part 1 merged:
    1. **Two of the three drift kinds have NO live inputs.** `skipped_pattern_streak` and `plan_deviation` need a **multi-session planned-vs-completed history**, but Atlas persists only *logged* history — past **plans are ephemeral client state**, never stored. The Missed-Lift memory the plan named (`services/coachMemory.js::detectMissedLifts` / `services/memoryModule.js::buildMemorySnapshot` `opts.missedLiftHistory`) **is never populated on any live path** (grep: no route/`index.js`/`liveIntelligence.js` caller passes `missedLiftHistory`). So those two kinds can't be fed live without a new plan-persistence layer (a larger, owner-adjacent data change). Only **`sandbag_persistence`** is live-computable today (it derives from `memory_patterns` / `detectPatterns` over logged rows).
    2. **The chat path computes no `coach_mode` yet.** B4 wired `selectCoachMode`/`register` on the **set-reaction** path only; the **chat register was explicitly deferred (B4-4)**. Wiring `challenge`/`reassure` into the chat voice therefore first requires introducing mode computation + register on the chat path — i.e. B5 Part 2 is entangled with the deferred B4-4 chat-register work, and with the chat-path mode **precedence** (recovery/tiredness must beat reassure; safety must beat challenge).
    3. **`sandbag_persistence` already partly reaches challenge.** `selectCoachMode` (B1) already maps `consistent_underperformance` → `challenge`; the incremental value of routing `detectDrift` for the sandbag kind specifically (vs just feeding `memory_patterns` when the chat path gains mode) is small until the plan-history kinds are feedable.
    - **Recommended scoping (PM-authority, for the Part-2 slice / owner awareness):** (a) FIRST land the chat-path mode/register foundation (resolve B4-4) so `challenge`/`reassure` have a home on the chat voice, with precedence tests (safety/recovery win); (b) wire the LIVE-computable triggers only — `sandbag_persistence`→challenge and `detectDiscouragement`→reassure with the zoom-out pack — since those inputs exist from logs; (c) file **plan-persistence** (store planned-vs-completed per session) as the explicit prerequisite for the two plan-history drift kinds, which stay dark until then. This keeps each Part-2 PR a tiny, honest diff instead of silently shipping drift kinds that can never fire. Not built this session — surfaced with evidence per the "stop and report a wrong premise" rule rather than worked around.
    - **✅ UPDATE (2026-07-10, owner-directed follow-up) — corrected B5 dependency graph:**
      - (a) **DONE — chat-path coach-MODE foundation shipped** (`services/chatCoachMode.js::deriveChatCoachMode` wired into `buildChatContext`; pure, tested; forwards `coach_mode` through the existing chat whitelist, register left null). This is the **mode** half of the deferred B4-4 only — per owner directive it adds NO drift/discouragement detection, NO plan persistence, NO register/profanity expansion. It is the single seam B5 Part 2 attaches `challenge`/`reassure` to. Precedence preserved: the route resolves tiredness/recovery before the LLM, so the chat mode only rides the non-tired turn.
      - (c) **FILED — plan-persistence routed to the Decision Desk (#952).** The two plan-history drift kinds (`skipped_pattern_streak`, `plan_deviation`) remain **BLOCKED** until persisted planned-vs-completed history exists — an **owner-reserved schema/persistence decision**, not to be built without approval. `#952` documents the minimum facts, storage options (append-only `Session_Plans` tab vs reconstruct-from-client vs descope), trust implications, migration impact, and the narrowest first PR.
      - (b) **STILL PENDING (now unblocked to build once authorized):** wire only the LIVE-computable triggers on the new mode seam — `sandbag_persistence`→challenge (or simply the existing `memory_patterns` path the foundation already carries) and `detectDiscouragement`→reassure with the zoom-out pack. Do **not** ship a partial B5a (drift wiring) unless a revised plan explicitly authorizes the sandbag-only split; `sandbag_persistence` may remain listed as currently computable.
      - **Corrected dependency graph:** `B5a`/`B5b` Part 2 → **depend on the chat-path coach-mode foundation** (now SHIPPED). `sandbag_persistence`→challenge and `detectDiscouragement`→reassure → **buildable** on the foundation (owner-authorized split pending). `skipped_pattern_streak` + `plan_deviation` → **BLOCKED on persisted plan history (#952, owner-reserved).** `ATLAS_COACH_PROFANITY` stays **OFF** until LT-010 (live mode/register/profanity validation) passes.
    - **✅ UPDATE (2026-07-10, owner-authorized B5a slice) — `sandbag_persistence`→challenge chat wiring SHIPPED.** Owner authorized ONLY the sandbag challenge slice as the next B5 PR (not combined with `detectDiscouragement`; no `skipped_pattern_streak`/`plan_deviation` until `Session_Plans` persistence exists; no register/profanity). It consumes the existing deterministic `memory_patterns.consistent_underperformance` signal, mapped to `challenge` through the merged chat coach-mode foundation (`deriveChatCoachMode`, #953), and adds the smallest prompt wiring: a **CHALLENGE MODE block** in `buildChatSystemPrompt` (state the pattern's numbers from `memory_patterns`, ask ONE open question, never lecture, hold the line on pushback without caving, no register/profanity escalation, never invent a pattern, fires ONLY when `coach_mode==='challenge'`). All other chat behavior unchanged. Failing prompt-rule tests first, then green (`test/coachPromptRules.test.js`). **Hard gate satisfied:** LT-007 recorded PASS (2026-07-09), so this may be promoted to live behavior — current-session truth still outranks personality. **`detectDiscouragement`→reassure remains a SEPARATE later PR** (after this is merged + validated, LT-007 PASS, and the chat-mode path is proven stable).
- ✅ **`[correctness]` discouragement engine trigger — PR-B5b Part 1 + Part 2 SHIPPED.** Part 1: pure `services/discouragementSignal.js` `detectDiscouragement(messageText, context)` → `{ discouraged, kind: frustration|negative_self_talk|stall_frustration, matched }` — explicit-phrase classes only (\b-anchored, negation + analytical-question guards, lift-name-collision-proof; pure tiredness never fires — it stays the recovery read's job). **Part 2 (SHIPPED, owner-authorized 2026-07-10) — wiring:** the chat route computes `detectDiscouragement(message)` and passes `discouraged` into `deriveChatCoachMode`; `selectCoachMode` gained an explicit-discouragement → `reassure` trigger (`coachMode.js`), and `buildChatSystemPrompt` (`services/coach.js`) gained a **REASSURE MODE block** gated on `coach_mode==='reassure'`: acknowledge in ONE clause, zoom out using ONLY snapshot facts (per-lift trends / `athlete_identity` streaks·tenure·PRs / the stalled lift's arc — cite, never invent), ONE concrete next move, **thin history = say less not warmer** (never fabricate progress), no "believe in yourself" filler, defer to pain/safety/recovery, no profanity, no write claims. **Precedence held:** safety/refuse/correct/challenge still outrank reassure (B1); the route resolves tiredness/recovery **before** the LLM so recovery beats reassure (pure tiredness never fires discouragement anyway). `discouragementSignal` removed from the wiring allowlist (now wired). Tests: `coachMode` (discouraged→reassure + precedence), `chatCoachMode` (derive discouraged→reassure), `coachPromptRules` (the reassure block), `api-smoke` (discouraged message → reassure; tiredness → recovery). **Hard gate satisfied:** LT-007 recorded PASS, so this may be promoted live. Explicit-phrases only — no sentiment model / no inferred emotional state.
  - **✅ UPDATE (2026-07-10, Owner Decision 1 / LT-011) — precedence CHANGED: explicit discouragement now overrides a standing challenge pattern for that message only.** Live LT-011 validation found reassure was **dormant** for any athlete with a standing `consistent_underperformance` pattern (challenge > reassure fired on every turn) and that a lift-naming discouragement message was swallowed by the deterministic lift-answer lane. Owner verdict: *"Explicit discouragement overrides a standing challenge pattern for that message only. Safety and recovery remain higher precedence."* Built as the expected small precedence change — `selectCoachMode` moves the explicit-discouragement `reassure` trigger **above** `challenge` (still below safety/refuse/correct; the layoff-return reassure is unchanged, staying below challenge), and the chat route now evaluates `detectDiscouragement` **before** the lift-answer lanes (bypassing them only for a discouraged, non-tired message) so a lift-naming discouragement reaches the reassure voice. **Message-scoped:** the standing pattern is never cleared, so the next ordinary turn challenges again. Safety (pain/form) and the route-level recovery/tiredness read still outrank reassure. No profanity, no write claims, no invented facts. Tests: `test/discouragementOverridesChallenge.test.js` (the 9 owner-required cases) + flipped precedence assertions in `coachMode`/`chatCoachMode` + two route tests in `api-smoke` (lift-naming discouragement → reassure; tiredness+discouragement → recovery). Full suite green. LT-011 record updated with the verdict. `ATLAS_COACH_PROFANITY` stays OFF.
- **Recorded readings (PM authority, documented in the module header; revisit in PR-B4 wiring review if wrong):** (1) a routine info-level `hold`/`load` rule decision does NOT map to `refuse` (mirrors `hasActionableRuleDecision` — info-level progression calls never break silence); `reject` at any severity, or `hold` at warning/error, refuses. (2) `PAIN_INJURY` maps to `safety` alongside `FORM_SAFETY` (pain is the highest safety class everywhere else). (3) `UNEXPECTED_EXCELLENCE` maps to `note`, not `praise` — praise stays reserved for an earned `beat`; if the owner wants positive-deviation praise, that is a one-line remap in PR-B4. (4) `new_ground` with a spent scarcity budget downgrades `celebrate` → `praise` (the PR-B3 fixture's expected downgrade lives here, not in B2).

## Session_Plans persistence lane (Decision Desk #952, Option A — owner-approved 2026-07-10)

Owner approved **Option A** (append-only, event-sourced `Session_Plans` tab) with a revised 13-column schema and frozen `event_type` (`plan_accepted | item_outcome | session_closeout`) + `outcome` (`planned | completed | skipped | substituted`) vocabularies. This is the data layer that unblocks the drift detector's `skipped_pattern_streak` / `plan_deviation` kinds. Narrow lane (do NOT combine skipped-pattern/plan-deviation detection into any of these; canonical lift codes only; no loads/reps/RIR; never `Log_Cleaned`; append-only; retry-safe via `idempotency_key`):

- ✅ **`closeout_status` vocab APPROVED (owner, 2026-07-10):** `finalized` = the athlete explicitly closed the session (NOT a completion claim, NOT proof rows were saved — completion is derived from item outcomes); `abandoned` = explicitly cancelled/discarded, no intent to continue. Never inferred from inactivity/timeout/redeploy/missing data; null on `plan_accepted`/`item_outcome` and while a session is open. Schema now fully frozen — the lane may proceed PR-B → PR-C without another owner check-in while the schema is unchanged and both stay narrow.
- ✅ **PR-B (SHIPPED) — idempotent append-only writer** `services/sessionPlanStore.js` (imports the PR-A builders; system-state write, no `write_id`, never the trust loop; **optional-tab 503/no-op until the owner creates the tab** — never auto-created). **Idempotency:** reads existing rows and matches by `idempotency_key` — an exact retry is skipped (no duplicate), and a key that matches an existing row with **different content** is a **revision collision that fails closed** (throws; the caller must bump `plan_version`) — the #957-review guard, preserved. Append-only; never mutates a prior event; writes only `plan_accepted` / explicit `item_outcome` / explicit `session_closeout`; never `Log_Cleaned`; no drift detection; no coach behavior. **Live capture wiring deferred to land with the PR-C reader** (so the whole data loop is wired + tested together); staged in the wiring allowlist (expires 2026-10-31; cap 11→12). Tests-first: `test/sessionPlanStore.test.js` (tab-missing no-op, per-item append, retry-idempotency, within-batch dedup, revision-collision fail-closed, item_outcome/substituted, closeout finalized|abandoned, header-row bootstrap; fake-sheets injected, no real I/O).
- ✅ **PR-C (SHIPPED) — reader/fold module + fixtures.** Pure `services/sessionPlanReader.js` — `foldSessionPlans(rows)` / `toPlannedVsCompleted(folded, {closedOnly})` / `readPlannedVsCompleted(rows)`. Folds append-only event rows by `(session_id + plan_version + plan_item_id)` **last-wins** into the exact `detectDrift` `plannedVsCompleted` shape (`[{date, planned:string[], completed:string[]}]`, newest last). **Completion derived from ITEM OUTCOMES, not `closeout_status`** (`completed` = the planned code for a `completed` item, the performed code for a `substituted` item; `skipped`/still-`planned` contribute nothing). Supports finalized/abandoned/partial/substituted/skipped/still-open (`status` per session; open sessions excluded from the default `closedOnly` drift view). **Fail closed:** a malformed event, or two rows sharing an `idempotency_key` with different content, marks that session `status:'error'` and drops it from the drift output. **Authoritative dedup:** identical duplicate `idempotency_key` rows collapse (the #958-review carry-over — a doubled write is invisible). PURE (no Sheets I/O); staged in the wiring allowlist (cap 12→13). Tests-first: `test/sessionPlanReader.test.js` (completion-from-outcomes, substituted-performed-code, all session states, last-wins correction, duplicate-key collapse, malformed + same-key-conflict fail-closed, drift-shape + newest-last, totality) — rows round-tripped through the PR-A builders.
  - **🛑 STOP-&-REPORT (2026-07-10, accepted by owner) — the single-narrow-PR premise was false; NO authoritative server-side capture boundary exists.** Evidence-based finding: the plan lifecycle (accept / skip / substitute / finalize / cancel) is decided entirely in the **browser** (`src/app/app.js` + `src/app/store.js` + `src/app/activeSession.js`); the server only ever sees logged **sets** (`Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`) keyed by `session_id`, with **no `plan_version`** and **no per-item plan identity** at any write point. `plan_version`/`plan_item_id` exist nowhere outside the unwired modules + schema. There is no `/api/plan/accept|close|cancel` route; the only explicit start affordance is the secondary intent-drawer "Start Session" (`app.js:2421`), and the primary Coach's Pick flow has no explicit acceptance. Capture therefore requires introducing a plan-identity model + explicit lifecycle protocol — **new product/data-model scope**, not a reuse-only wiring PR. Did not guess, invent endpoints, or ship an inference shim (`CLAUDE.md` "stop and report a wrong premise").
  - **✅ OPTION A AUTHORIZED (owner, 2026-07-10) — explicit Session_Plans lifecycle capture as a multi-PR lane.** Drafts stay ephemeral; a plan becomes authoritative only via an **explicit acceptance action** that establishes persisted identity (`plan_version` + immutable `plan_item_id`); item outcomes + closeout carry that identity explicitly; **never derive** identity/outcomes from logged sets, transcript text, inactivity, browser close, or missing state; Session_Plans stays a sidecar (never corrupts/duplicates the workout write; never `Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`; no "remembered" claim unless the append succeeded). Substitution keeps the original `plan_item_id` + records `performed_lift_code`; reordering execution never rewrites the accepted plan; extra/off-plan lifts never become planned items; a materially different accepted plan gets a new `plan_version`. **Spec:** `docs/SESSION_PLANS_CAPTURE_SPEC.md`.
    - **PR-G — explicit item-outcome capture (split by owner 2026-07-10: G1 skipped/substituted now; G2 completed via an explicit button).**
    - ✅ **PR-I — canary + reader verification COMPLETE (2026-07-11).** An automated live-write canary validated the full lifecycle (`accept` / `completed` / `substituted` with `performed_lift_code` / `skipped` / `finalized` closeout) plus idempotent replay (0 new rows), invalid-payload rejection (400, 0 rows), and an `abandoned` lifecycle in the real `Session_Plans` tab under one identity; canary rows were administratively removed and `ATLAS_SESSION_PLANS_WRITE` was enabled for real shadow capture (full evidence in the "Session_Plans PR-I — production-validated + mid-plan Done RESOLVED" entry in the reference section above — this line previously read `⏸ pending`, which contradicted that record; reconciled to it). **Remaining (deferred, owner-gated):** enabling the `skipped_pattern_streak` / `plan_deviation` detectors that consume the captured plan history.
    - **✅ OWNER DECISIONS (PR-D approved with amendments, 2026-07-10):** (1) **ID issuance — client-generated OPAQUE UUID-backed IDs** (`pv_<uuid>` / `pi_<uuid>`), minted at the explicit acceptance action before the request, stored in active session state + snapshot, reused on retry/reload, a **new `plan_version` per explicit acceptance**, **never** content-derived and **never** regenerated from the exercise array; use the platform crypto UUID generator, never timestamps/`Math.random`. `plan_version` is the opaque identity of one immutable accepted-plan revision (not a counter). (2) **Acceptance affordance — a distinct "Start this plan" button on the rendered plan card** is the v1 authoritative boundary; do NOT repurpose a generic "Start Session" that only opens the coach surface; it starts the workout regardless of flag/sidecar and never claims persistence unless `captured:true`. (3) **Plan replacement — DEFERRED:** implicit `replace_plan` is NOT a new accepted revision (no `plan_accepted`, existing snapshot unmutated). Spec updated accordingly.
  - **Later UX item (filed 2026-07-10) — `Add explicit "Replace current plan" confirmation`** `[ux]`: the implicit coach `replace_plan` auto-apply (`src/app/app.js:2190`) currently stays a draft and is never persisted. A future narrow PR adds an explicit "Replace current plan" confirmation; when confirmed it mints a **new `plan_version`** and a **new immutable item set** (the prior accepted snapshot is retained append-only, never mutated). Outside the PR-E→PR-I lane.
  - **Carry-over from the #958 (PR-B) review — address in PR-C / the capture wiring:** (1) `sessionPlanStore._existingByKey` swallows a Sheets read error and returns an empty map, so a transient read failure during a genuine retry bypasses the cross-retry idempotency guard and could re-append — harmless given the reader's last-wins fold, but the **reader's fold must be the authoritative dedup** (collapse duplicate `idempotency_key` rows, last-wins) so a doubled write is invisible downstream. (2) `sessionPlanStore._contentEqual` compares MORE fields than `idempotencyKey` hashes (`session_date`, `planned_order`, `movement_pattern`, and `planned_lift_code` for `item_outcome`) — the safe fail-closed direction, but the **capture wiring must reconstruct each event's metadata deterministically across retries** (same `session_date` format, `planned_order`, `movement_pattern`) or benign drift will trip a revision-collision throw. Pin both with reader fixtures (duplicate-key collapse; metadata-drift determinism).

## Soul Plan PR-B4 — split into safe slices (2026-07-10, PM authority)

The plan packages B4 as one PR, but it is genuinely 3+ concerns landing in the two most-restricted files (`services/coach.js`, `routes/coachOps.js`) — and the Atlas PR contract mandates tiny focused diffs there. Split under PM authority (derivable from "one concern per PR / tiny PRs / stop if a change spreads"), with the hard safety rule that **the profanity permission and its deterministic suppressor ship in the same slice** (never forward a permission without its guard). Slices keep the plan's B4 semantics intact across them:

- ✅ **B4-3 (SHIPPED) — profanity + suppressor + trust gate, together:** `profanity_ok` now forwarded by `sanitizeRegister`; persona core carries the D1 profanity rule (gated on `register.profanity_ok`, hard-banned in safety/pain/correction/uncertainty); `finalizeCoachVoice` (`routes/coachOps.js`) gained the deterministic register suppressor (`coach.findRegisterViolations`: profanity tokens without the grant, or celebration/PR vocab outside celebrate/praise → prose suppressed to the renderer line). **Trust gate CLOSED:** profanity is gated on `confirmTodayNewGround` (`services/liveIntelligence.js`) — an ENGINE-side recompute of today's progression verdict — so a forged client `rec.progression_verdict` can't reach the cell. **Production staging:** profanity is OFF by default; the route passes `ownerPrefs.profanity_enabled = (process.env.ATLAS_COACH_PROFANITY === 'on')`, so the owner activates it on Render after the LT-010 flight-recorder review (the ratified D1 calibration file stays `enabled:true`; the env gate defaults it off — the `ATLAS_COACH_ENGINE`/`ATLAS_INTENT_ROUTER` staging pattern). Property test pins profanity to the celebrate×max×scarcity-clear×enabled cell; suppressor + engine-gate unit-tested; LT-010 filed. **Residual client-trust `[correctness]` (raised by the #948 review; bounded, not fully closed):** `confirmTodayNewGround`'s band is server-recomputed, but `todayTop` comes from `facts.todaySets` — the just-logged set, which is NOT in the sheet yet and is therefore inherently **client-asserted**. So the engine gate raises the bar (a forger must claim a *plausible* PR weight, and an absurd one — e.g. 99999 — is rejected by the 1.5×-ceiling plausibility cap) but does **not** fully close client trust: a client claiming a believable new-ground weight can still reach the profanity cell once the env flag is on. Impact is low (single-owner, read-only endpoint, staged OFF, worst case one swear in the owner's own reply) and the layer-3 suppressor is the other net. A **full** close would require reacting only to a **written** set (post-save), which is a larger change to the coach-reaction timing — deferred. The corrected claim: the gate is *defense-in-depth that bounds* the forgery, not a complete closure. LT-010's live probe should include a forged-`todaySets` weight, not just a forged verdict.

**Deferred follow-up `[correctness]`:** complete the `selectCoachMode` input set at the set-reaction call site (`rule_decisions`/`verdict`/`memory_patterns`/`layoff` are not passed there — `safety` still fires via `note_trigger`, and profanity can't leak on a non-celebrate mode, but a `reject` rule won't reach its `refuse` register floor). Also: the chat-path register is still unwired (set/block path only) — `buildChatContext` may compute mode/register in a follow-up, or file as B4-4.
  - ✅ **RESOLVED in B4-3 (was a 🔒 BLOCKING trust requirement, raised by the Claude reviewer on #946):** the precondition — never gate the forwarded `profanity_ok` on the client-derivable mode — is met. B4-3 gates profanity on `confirmTodayNewGround` (`services/liveIntelligence.js`), an ENGINE-side recompute of today's progression verdict, so a forged client `rec.progression_verdict` can no longer reach the certified profanity cell. **Genuine residual `[correctness]` (not blocking):** `todayTop` still comes from the client-asserted just-logged set — bounded by the 1.5× plausibility ceiling but not fully closed (a full close would react only to a *written* set post-save; see the B4-3 SHIPPED note above) — and the `selectCoachMode` input set is still incomplete at the set-reaction call site (`rule_decisions`/`verdict`/`memory_patterns`/`layoff` not passed; `safety` still fires via `note_trigger`, but a `reject` rule decision won't reach its conservative `refuse` register floor). Tracked by the "Deferred follow-up" note above.
- **Chat-path register (deferred within B4):** the set/block path is wired here. The chat voice's "mode" for a free-form reply is a genuine design question (mostly silent/educate/nod) — wire it in B4-2/B4-3 alongside the persona block, or file as B4-4 if it needs its own shape. The chat sanitizer already carries the `coach_mode`/`register` whitelist (forwarding null until computed).
  - **✅ UPDATE (2026-07-10) — the chat-path coach-MODE half of B4-4 is now SHIPPED** (`services/chatCoachMode.js`, wired into `buildChatContext`): the chat context now carries a real engine `coach_mode` (today `challenge` on a recurring underperformance pattern, else `silent`) instead of a permanent null. Per owner directive this foundation grants **no register** (register stays null on the chat path) and adds no new detection — the **register** half of B4-4 (what routine/elevated/max license on a free-form reply, and any profanity gate) remains deferred and owner-gated, and `ATLAS_COACH_PROFANITY` stays OFF until LT-010.

## Soul Plan PR-B3 → PR-B4 reconciliation + a possible live-path latent bug (2026-07-10)

- **`[correctness]` PR-B4 must reconcile warm-up handling in the session top.** `services/celebrationScarcity.js` excludes note-tagged warm-ups from the event-day top (matching `progressionBand`), but the LIVE coach verdict path `analyzeLift` (`services/analytics.js:720-722`) computes `progressionTop` filtering only `isPositiveFinite(weight)` — it INCLUDES a tagged heavy warm-up while its band excludes them. So on a session whose max weight is a tagged warm-up, the scarcity signal suppresses the "event" while the live `new_ground` verdict could fire. When PR-B4 wires scarcity alongside the live verdict, do not assume byte-parity on that edge. **Recommended reconciliation (owner/Codex-gated — it changes a live verdict):** align `analyzeLift`'s `progressionTop` to exclude warm-ups too (`!isWarmupNote(r.notes)`), which is the more-correct reading — a tagged warm-up must never manufacture a working-weight PR, the exact reason warm-up tagging exists. That is a separate trust-sensitive analytics change, not done in B3. (Raised by the Claude reviewer on #945.)

## Soul Plan PR-B2 — recorded reading (2026-07-10)

- **D1's second certified profanity moment (earned comeback beat) is intensity-only in PR-B2.** `grantRegister` gives a comeback-beat praise MAX intensity (owner-enabled via `config/coaching/register.calibration.json` → `max_on_comeback_beat_praise`), but `profanity_ok` stays celebrate-cell-only — B2's own iron invariant pins `profanity_ok ⟹ mode==='celebrate'`, and there is no engine-certified "comeback beat" fact yet for the grant to trust. When PR-B4/B6 wire a deterministic comeback-beat signal, deciding whether that cell also earns profanity per D1 is a one-line calibration+schema change — do it consciously then, not silently now.

## Coach voice architecture — findings from PR-A1 review (2026-07-09)

Five items filed per `docs/COACH_VOICE_ARCHITECTURE_REVIEW_2026-07-09.md` §6 and `docs/SOUL_PLAN_V1.md` PR-A1 scope. Do not act on these out of sequence — the Soul Plan dependency chain governs.

- **Persona-core extraction** `[correctness]` — `services/coach.js:48` ("sharp, encouraging") contradicts the owner-ratified `:1258` logbook-keeper identity. Extract `services/coachPersonaCore.js`; wire `:1258` into all five prompt builders; delete `:48` placeholder. Authorized by `docs/SOUL_PLAN_V1.md` PR-A2. **Dependency: none** (PR-A1 is this doc).

- **Persona doc–code drift reconciliation** `[polish]` — six deterministic copy sources (`coachVoiceRenderer.js`, `deterministicCoachRenderer.js`, `setEffortCopy.js`, `src/app/coachVoiceTemplates.js`, `VERDICT_VARIANTS`/`coachOpener` in `coach-conversation.js`, `coachPolish.js`) may carry phrasing that contradicts the ratified persona core. Review-only pass after PR-A2 lands; actual copy fixes are a later-pass item. **Dependency: PR-A2** (persona core must exist first).
  - **PR-A2 review-pass result (2026-07-09, no edits made):** the six sources are broadly consistent with the new `services/coachPersonaCore.js` — no emoji, no pet names, no gym-bro hype ("beast mode"/"crushing it"), no exclamation stacking, and the verdict copy is conclusion-first, unrounded, and judged against the lifter's own target (aligned with the core). One phrase to reconcile in the later copy pass: `src/app/coach-conversation.js` uses "solid session" / "Solid session" as plan-intro and closeout framing (lines ~586, ~804, ~1497) — "solid session" is on the core's banned empty-filler list, though here it reads as neutral plan framing ("here's a solid session for today") rather than empty praise of a logged set. Borderline-but-tolerable: the `hard`-verdict variant "A grinder — … Quality work." is engine-gated to a genuinely hard set, so it is not ordinary-compliance praise. `coachPolish.js` preserves numbers and degrades to the original line — no persona conflict. Net: no trust or hype violation found; the only cosmetic reconciliation candidate is the "solid session" framing. Fix belongs to the later copy pass, not PR-A2.

- ✅ **Athlete-identity facts object — SHIPPED (Soul Plan PR-A7, 2026-07-10)** `[correctness]` — pure `services/athleteIdentity.js` (`buildAthleteIdentity(logRows, { asOf })`: tenure/first-session, per-lift dated PR progressions ≤3 + current_best, weekly streak + trailing-8-week rate, longest gap + days-since, 60-day milestones; warm-ups excluded from PRs via `warmupTag`, thin history <3 sessions → mostly-null, asOf injected — no clock). Forwarded as `athlete_identity` through NEW explicit whitelists in `sanitizeFacts`/`sanitizeChatContext` (`sanitizeAthleteIdentity`, bounds 8 lifts/3 PRs/6 milestones, literal-date-only); computed server-side ONLY from rows the routes already fetch (set-reaction: the enrichment `allLog`, always-overwritten so a client object never reaches the model; chat: `buildChatContext`'s own `logRows`) — zero additional Sheets reads, introspection-guarded. Persona core gained the shared **ATHLETE HISTORY — cited, never invented** iron rule (rule 6). Tests: `test/athleteIdentity.test.js` + `test/athleteIdentityForwarding.test.js`. Original finding retained: `detectRecentPrs`/`buildProgressSummary` remain unforwarded — the identity object supersedes them as the longitudinal whitelist surface.
  - **Follow-up `[polish]` (PR-A7, documented limitation):** the set-reaction path computes `athlete_identity` only when the client sends a `liftCode` (that is when the route fetches `allLog` for enrichment). A liftCode-less set reaction gets `athlete_identity: null` rather than a new Sheets read — per the read-budget guard ("no hot-route read increase"). If a real surface turns out to need the story without a liftCode, decide then whether the read is worth it; do not add it silently.
  - **Follow-up `[polish]` (PR-A7, wording surface not wired):** the deterministic templated fallbacks (`coachVoiceTemplates.js`, `coachVoiceRenderer.js`) don't cite identity facts — LLM-down notes stay ahistorical. Fine today (deterministic copy invents nothing); wire a cited-fact variant only if a later Soul Plan PR names it.

- ✅ **Structured goals — SHIPPED (Soul Plan PR-B8a, 2026-07-11)** `[correctness]` — the one identity fact the log can't derive: what the lifter trains TOWARD. Owner storage decision **D8 (Constraints-tab row convention)**: a `goal`-kind Constraints row whose `target` cell is `"<lift_code> <target_weight> [target_reps]"` (e.g. `bench_press 215`). New pure `services/athleteGoals.js` (`readAthleteGoals(rows)`) parses `goal` rows to the frozen schema `{lift_code, target_weight, target_reps?, set_date, note?}`, degrade-to-skip on any malformed row, never throws, no I/O/clock. Forwarded as `athlete_goals` through a NEW frozen whitelist (`sanitizeAthleteGoals` — bounds ≤12 goals, weight 0<w≤2000, reps 1–100, literal-date-only, note≤200, unknown keys dropped) in `sanitizeChatContext`; the chat route derives it from the **Constraints rows it already fetched** (`coachOps.js` `buildChatContext`, threaded via `constraintRows`) — **zero new Sheets read** (introspection-guarded, single `getSheetRows('Constraints')`). Persona core gained the shared **GOAL PROXIMITY — cited from both ends** iron rule (rule 7: a near-goal claim needs the goal in `athlete_goals` AND a current number in the facts, else no claim); the chat prompt describes the `athlete_goals` fact. No schema/contract change (the 5-col `Constraints` tab and `kind` vocabulary are untouched — `goal` rows are simply data the write route / `sanitizeConstraint` / resolver all correctly ignore; goals are hand-seeded owner data). Tests: `test/athleteGoals.test.js` (reader) + `test/athleteGoalsForwarding.test.js` (whitelist round-trip, persona/prompt grounding, zero-new-read guard). Read-only from the coach's perspective; no write path.

Verified against `main`, the **coach voices do not consume `assembleState`** — `stateAssembly` feeds the recommend/generate (Brain) routes, while the chat coach builds its snapshot in `buildChatContext` with its own Constraints read. So goals were hydrated where they actually reach the voice (the chat route's already-fetched rows, zero new read), which preserves the concern ("the coach knows your goals, cited never invented"). No `stateAssembly` change was made.

Wire it only if a surface needs goal proximity at set-reaction time, deciding the read tradeoff then. (2) **`stateAssembly` / Brain-snapshot goals** — adding a `getGoals` reader + `athlete_goals` to `assembleState` + `knownKeys` is architecture-consistent and needed when the Brain / PR-B6 Moments (goal-proximity moment, D6 F1(a)) consume goals; not built here (future-consumer scope). (3) goal **EDITING** surface + seeding the owner's real goals (215 Bench / 215 Back Squat) is owner data, not app code (per the spec).

If the owner later wants the set-reaction voice to word raised `rule_decisions` directly, wiring that new fact channel into `sanitizeFacts` is a scoped feature PR (new capability), not a fold — build it consciously then.

Not an LT-010 Part 1 gate (that gate covers the set-reaction `coach_mode`/`register`, which PASSED) and the challenge precedence is owner-ratified — do not re-litigate unilaterally.
